# City Legends

> **LICENCE WARNING (owner, read this):** these car models are **NC (non-commercial)** and the game sells Robux items, so they are risky: Dearborn C346 (Focus RS), Stealth-Apex V10 Halo and Halo Circuit (LFAs), Z223 Royale V12 (Maybach S-Class), all Ddiaz Design, plus the Lamborghini / Ferrari ones listed further down. Their part names (`Rs:SK_...`, `MI_...`, `Geo_lodA`) look ripped from a racing game. The X167 Royale and Classico 5000 are CC-BY (OK with credit, in the credits), though the X167's parts are from a game mod. The call is yours. The game still works if one model is swapped later.

A Roblox night-city driving game. The code is in this vault, folder `Games/CityLegends`; its `CLAUDE.md` has the full technical notes.

Lessons from the top 10 driving games (2026-10-30): [[Research - Driving games]] (read before planning features).
- Owner picked (2026-10-30) improvements 1, 2, 4-10 from the research list (not 3, performance tuning, yet): Legends Path milestones, 7-day streak with a car on day 7 (replaces the flat daily gift), race lobbies and duels, 3 daily tasks, live map markers, meet spot + photo mode, radio stations, brand levels, 5-minute test drive. **All 9 built (2026-10-30):** GOALS button (Path, Daily, Streak), race lobbies at the race rings + duels (U next to a driver), live bank/casino/race markers on the minimap, LEGENDS MEET car park behind Legends Motors ($300/min per other car parked there, up to 5) + photo mode (O or Settings), 4 radio stations (Settings > Radio), brand levels (garage shows BRAND LV; cheaper Customs, trims sooner), TEST DRIVE button at Legends Motors (5 min, quarter pay). Duel/pot still need a 2-player test.

## Real car prices, real part prices, harder cash (2026-11-07, `Prompts/Real car prices and harder cash.md`)
**Owner, your calls:** the 3 below.
1. **Robux items:** Starter Pack ($50,000), Cash $25,000 and Cash $100,000 are now worth about 2, 1 and 4 hours of play (they were worth about 20 minutes to an hour). Nothing changed: they have no ids yet.
2. **Houses, penthouses, hotel suites, gems, VIP cards:** prices not changed. At about $28K an hour the first house ($150,000) takes 6 hours, the first penthouse ($750,000) 27 hours, the $12M and $25M ones 400 to 900 hours. Keep them as long-term dreams, or cut them?
3. **The car market:** it works in percentages of the car, so a good flip on a dear car now pays more than an hour of play. Not in the simulation, not changed.

- Velocité Noir Éclair Pure: $11,000,000 -> $4,000,000
- Velocité Noir Éclair: $9,500,000 -> $3,250,000

|---|---|---|
| Cut-up base | $120 | $18 |
| Driving at 90 mph | $5.00/s | $0.75/s |
| Race gold / silver / bronze / finish | $9,000 / 5,000 / 2,500 / 1,000 | $1,350 / 750 / 375 / 150 |
| Delivery job per stud, per second left | $7, $40 | $0.60, $5 |

| Chip, all chips | $2,500, $100,000 | $375, $15,000 |

| Daily streak days 1-6 | $5K to 35K | $750 to 5,250 |
| Daily tasks | $5-8K | $500-800 (x1 to x3 by level) |
| Weekly gift | $150,000 | $22,500 |
| Level-up cash | $500 + 250 a level | $50 + 25 a level |
| Level gift, chest (x level) | $2,000, $8,000 | $40, $160 |
| SPIN price, value of a free one | $50,000, $17,500 | $5,000, about $1,900 |
| SUPER SPIN price, value of a free one | $1,000,000, about $950,000 | $30,000, about $14,000 |
| Legends Path up to $8,000, above | as they were | x 0.4, x 0.15 |
| Hotel suite income, tower rent | $2,000-30,000 a minute, $12-160K a day | $300-4,500 a minute, $1,800-24,000 a day |
| Fines, escape bounties, meet bonus, flip-off prize | | x 0.15 (the 10% busted fine stays) |
| Day-7 streak car, Mid at or under | $120,000 | $50,000 |
| Chauffeur contracts | old car prices | the cars' new prices |
XP is unchanged: a cut-up's XP used to come from its cash, so that number moved the other way.

**A normal hour, before -> after (worked out from the numbers in the code, not played; only driving was measured in Play: $0.39 a second at 41 mph, as the sum says):**
| Way to earn | Mix | Before | After |
|---|---|---|---|
| Driving | 30 min at 80 mph | $8,000 | $1,200 |
| Cut-ups | 150 | $21,600 | $3,240 |
| Races | 2 silver | $10,000 | $1,500 |
| Delivery jobs | 2 | $22,600 | $1,985 |
| Drift | 4 | $2,000 | $300 |
| Jumps | 4 | $9,600 | $1,440 |
| Bank heist | 0.4 | $16,800 | $1,680 |
| Police escapes | 0.5 | $400 | $60 |
| **All of it** | | **about $91,000** | **about $11,400** |
Back to back, jobs paid about 10 times anything else, so they dropped most. Gifts, tasks, spins, levels and goals add about $14,000 an hour at 1.5 hours a day.

**Simulated progress (`tools/progression_test.luau`, a normal player, 1.5 hours a day, spending nothing):**
| Goal | Target | Here |
|---|---|---|
| First part ($750) | under 10 min | minute 1 (the $5,000 you start with) |
| First car, $30K | 30-60 min | 30 min |
| Good Mid, $100K | 3-5 h | 2.5 h |
| Top Mid, $250K | 8-12 h | 8 h |
| First High, $500K | 15-25 h | 17 h |
| $1M | 35-60 h | 31.5 h |
| $3M | 100-150 h | 104.5 h |
All within 25% of the range. Three run a little quick on purpose: better to tighten later than lose players.

**Tiers moved High -> Mid (the price is under $150K):** Horizon R35, British Vanguard Vanta V12, British Vanguard Vela V12, Rosso Rampante F131, Precision Type 42 V10, Speedmark P11, Enfurecido L140 Spyder, Rosso Rampante F149 Riviera. The Tier sets Customs prices, rewards and traffic. The W177 Turbo S ($86,000, a Starter) stays: not on sale yet.

**Other changes:**
- `Cars.lua`: `Price`, `PriceSource`, and `StatPrice` (the old price: a car's fallback weight came from its price, so stats and PI don't move). Trims still cost x 1.3 and x 1.7 of the base.
- The free Orient Voltra now shows its real price ($38,000) at the dealer. Whoever has it keeps it; the three starter picks stay free.
- Retired cars keep their old prices: not on sale.
- The dealer shows PRICE · NEW, USED or COLLECTOR. A trim shows its base car's tag.
- Old saves: all cars and cash stay. A spare part saved with a price factor over 1.5 is kept, at 1.5.
- Market: `HighFromPrice` $600K -> $300K (real prices are lower).

**Part prices:** real aftermarket price x 2.5 (`PartPriceMult`). Real ranges per slot: `PartData.Real`. Rarity sets the place in the range: Common the bottom, Legendary the top. Parts of $2,000 and more (real price) cost up to x 1.5 on cars from $500K, reaching it at $1.1M; smaller parts cost the same on every car. CP prices unchanged. The full list is below.

**Checked:** in Play on a test save (a fresh save, then put back): the first-car claim, buying the Halo Circuit at the dealer ($1,700,000 charged), a part on the 350Z and on the Circuit, the dealer's tag. Lune: carlevels, goals, market, world, graph, progression ALL OK; `luau-lsp` clean. `chauffeur_test` stops on a `workspace` error in `ChauffeurRoute`: not from this job (it needs a fake `workspace` in the test). **Not checked in Play:** Customs and the wheels with the new prices (a spin needs the desk), the market, a real old save.

**Rough prices (one source or asking prices only):** Sovereign Grand Tourer, Tri-Star Silber W177 Turbo S, Rocket W463 800, Velocité Noir Éclair, Enfurecido L147 Roadster, Enfurecido LB744, Enfurecido LB834 ZVX 63, Stuttgart 982, Apex JZX100 Tourer, Apex J300.

## Working lamps on the hypercars (2026-11-07, `Prompts/Proper working headlights.md`)
**Hypercars only for now (owner, 2026-11-07).** The other 16 new models keep their old lights until the owner says.
Pictures: [[Lamps - LFA low beam.jpg]], [[Lamps - LFA high beam.jpg]], [[Lamps - LB834 rear, all lamps.jpg]], [[Lamps - LB744 rear, all lamps.jpg]], [[Lamps - ZVX rear, all lamps.jpg]], [[Lamps - Eclair Super Sport rear, all lamps.jpg]], [[Lamps - Origine rear and phone button.jpg]], [[Lamps - Origine by day, DRL.jpg]], [[Lamps - rain at night, a car facing you.jpg]]. In the "all lamps" shots the indicators and reverse lamps were forced on to check where they sit.
- **Controls:** L = next mode: Auto, Low, High, Off. Hold L = high beam while held. Two quick taps = a short flash. Phones: a button by GAS (hidden on other cars). The LIGHTS word on the HUD is green on low, blue on high.
- **Auto:** the strips glow with the engine on; low beam at dusk and in tunnels.
- **How:** `client/CarLamps.lua`, numbers in `Config.Lamps`, shells `LampFx_...` in `CarModels` cut from each model's own lamp pieces (`tools/lamps`). Right side mirrored from the left. Real lights on your car and the nearest 6 (3 on Medium, none on Low, 12 on Ultra), none behind the camera; shadows on your own low beam from High graphics.
- **Limits:** a SpotLight stops at 60 studs, so high beam adds a third light 48 studs up the road. The beams start just ahead of the nose, level with the lamps: from the lamp itself they lit the bonnet. A separate DRL strip isn't split out: DRL is the bright lamp pieces at a lower glow.
- **Traffic, police, parked cars:** no change. None use these models, and traffic has no lights (owner, 2026-11-02).

| Car | Headlights | Tail and brake | Reverse | Indicators | Note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Stealth-Apex V10 Halo | ok | ok | ok | ok | the lens's inner part and outer part |
| V10 Halo Circuit | ok | ok | ok | ok | as the Halo |
| Enfurecido LB834 | ok | ok | ok | ok | own amber segments, inner red chip for reverse |
| LB834 ZV | ok | ok | ok | ok | as the LB834 |
| LB834 ZVX | ok | ok, new shell | ok | ok | was flat rectangles; lamps from the ZVX 63 |
| LB834 ZVX 63 | ok | ok | ok | ok | |
| Enfurecido LB744 | ok | ok, new shell | ok | ok | only a small low lens glowed before |
| Velocite Noir Origine | ok | ok | ok | ok | rear rings from the Super Sport model, moved to fit |
| Origine Sport, Origine Top | ok | ok | ok | ok | outer ring amber, inner ring white |
| Eclair | ok | ok | ok | ok | own amber piece at the back |
| Eclair Pure, Eclair Super Sport | ok | ok | ok | ok | |

- **Where reverse and rear indicators sit is partly my pick:** on models with no separate piece they use a slice of the tail lens (inner for reverse, outer for indicators), from the look of the model, not a photo of the real lamp.
- **Old cars:** the M5 F90 checked: L toggles as before, no Beam attribute, no phone button. The code only runs on the 13.
- **Checked in Play at night, PC size (forced touch HUD for the button's place):** modes, hold not tested by hand, blinkers left, reverse, day DRL, rain haze, another car's lights and glare on a test copy. FPS on the highway at night with one test car: 56 (day 58). **Not tested:** flash by double tap, a real phone, 2 real players, a tunnel, the 3 usual FPS spots, Customs headlight colour.

## One Urus, two 458s (2026-11-07, `Prompts/One Urus and only two 458s.md`)
Pictures: [[Dealer - one Brava.jpg]], [[Dealer - F142 line.jpg]].
- **Urus:** `lambrini_urusp` is now "Enfurecido Brava" (the Performante model, same stats, $1.9M), one car in its line, no trims. The old `lambrini_urus` is retired, named "Enfurecido Brava (old)", model kept in `CarModels`.
- **Save swap:** `Cars.Swapped` + `swapCars` in `PlayerData`: on load everything on the old id moves to the new one (level, XP, CP, parts, tune, builds, look, selected car, house bays, showcase, favourites, market stash, chauffeur contract). A part the new car can't take goes to My Parts.
- **Owns both:** keeps the car with the higher level. The other car's parts go to My Parts, and the CP its levels earned are added. No cash back for the second car (the prompt didn't ask for it).
- **Chauffeur:** the fleet's Urus is now `lambrini_urusp`, contract $1.9M (the fleet's prices match the cars').
- **458:** the Italia was already retired and stays in owners' garages. The F142 line is the Spider and Speciale, in that order.
- **Checked:** dealer in Play (one Brava, F142 with 2), `carlevels_test` with 3 fake saves (old only, both with each one the better), market and goals tests. How many owners moved is unknown: nothing is published, and the count needs a live run.

## New imports: LFA, LFA Circuit, Maybachs, Countach 5000 (2026-11-07, `Prompts/New imports - LFA, Maybachs, Countach.md`)
**Licences:** NC (non-commercial, risky in a game that sells Robux): Stealth-Apex V10 Halo, Halo Circuit (CC-BY-NC-SA), Z223 Royale V12 (CC-BY-NC), all Ddiaz Design, and the Dearborn C346 above. Their part names (`Kit0_Badge_Geo_lodA`, `_lodA`, `Lexus_LFAGold...`) look ripped from a racing game. OK with credit (CC-BY): X167 Royale (Black Snow), Enfurecido Classico 5000 (Ddiaz Design); both in `Config.Credits`. The GLS file is a game mod's export (BeamNG names, `etk800`, `amdb11`): the licence says CC-BY but its parts may not be the uploader's to give.

| Car | Price, level, class | Triangles |
|---|---|---|
| Stealth-Apex V10 Halo (`lexis_lfa`, same id, price) | $3,000,000, S1 737 | 215k |
| Stealth-Apex V10 Halo Circuit (`lexis_lfa_circuit`, **new**, suggested) | $3,600,000 (suggested), S2 758; 202 mph, 0-60 3.5, 563 hp, 1450 kg, wheelbase 2.605 | 229k |
| Tri-Star Silber Z223 Royale V12 (`bentz_s680`) | $1,600,000, B 502 | 219k |

| Enfurecido Classico 5000 (`lambrini_countach`, renamed from 400) | $2,500,000, B 563 | 196k |

- **Clusters:** `tools/lambo/cluster_lfa_maybach.py` (styles `LiveLFA`, `LiveMaybach`), `tools/lambo/countach_art.py` (two big dials at 800 px on panels, needles live). The photos are only 480 and 596 px wide, so everything is drawn fresh and placed by them, no pieces cut from the photos. Maybach: mark left out of both dials, "Media" for the Russian word.
- **Moves:** LFA: rev needle, speed, gear (N lights beside it), oil pressure bar with the revs, water bar when worked hard, trip lines, clock. Maybachs: both needles, digital speed, gear letters (R N D with the big letter), parked P, range, clock; the speedometer's numbers follow km/h or mph. Temperatures, fuel bars don't move.
- **Doors:** LFA, LFA Circuit, S-Class: one body, none separate. The GLS and the Countach have separate door pieces in the file, but the game has no door system yet (Part 4 of `Better car models...` was never built), so no scissor doors: not done.
- **Logos out:** LFA: hood badge, tail badge, steering hub and spoke, plates, the LFA word on the brakes and tyres. S-Class: hood star (a plain chrome button stays), boot star, wheel caps, hub, plates. GLS: hood star, tail star, wheel caps, letters, hub (the file's star is in the same part as other chrome: `cut_islands` in `finish_car.py`). Countach: shields on the dials, nose badge, wheel caps, plate, engine lettering.

LFA: black cabin with red seat centres (`tools/lambo/seat_two_tone.py`), red kept from its own palette. S-Class: the model's own quilted cream cabin.

Steering pieces are told apart by their longest side (the box in Blender is tilted). `CarLookCopy` swaps a rebuilt pack in and keeps the surfaces. A GLS collapse thinning left lumps on the chrome, so only hidden parts are thinned; 474k -> 384k. `LOW_ARMS` in `CarSkin`: on these five cars the hands sit at 9 and 3 and the arms lower, because the old line covered the cluster (owner's rule; other cars unchanged). `LoadAsset` works when `insert_asset` says "Failed to load package asset" (a just-uploaded big pack).

Lune tests all OK (world, graph, carlevels, goals, market; the market test's `stash capped` line failed once in 5 runs, it depends on chance), `luau-lsp` no errors. **Not checked:** the Countach's lamps closely, phone size, a real phone, 2 players, buying the Circuit at the dealer. ## Workshop and Part Shop (2026-11-06, `Prompts/Better upgrade area and part shop.md`)
Pictures: [[Workshop - upgrade screen.jpg]], [[Workshop - Part Shop.jpg]], [[Workshop - engine swaps.jpg]], [[Workshop - tune and builds.jpg]], [[Workshop - dyno run.jpg]], [[Workshop - phone size.jpg]]; next to the references: [[Workshop - reference and ours.jpg]], [[Part Shop - reference and ours.jpg]]. All parts and prices: [[Parts list]].
- **Where:** `client/Workshop.lua` (the room, the screen, the shop, TUNE, the dyno), `shared/PartData.lua` (slots, parts, 43 engines, old-save map), `CarStats` (the numbers, `fits`, `partPrice`, `dyno`), `server/CarLevels.lua` (buy, install, uninstall, sell, setup, builds, drops), `client/CarUpgrades.lua` (the cache). Icons: `tools/part_icons.py` -> `client/PartArt.lua` (74 white PNGs: 65 drawn + 9 engine-layout badges).
- **The room:** built on this screen only at y 3400, like the dealer's stage. The real car (the player's paint) on a two-post lift over a red ring, tool chests, tyre racks, engine stands, strip lights, polished concrete, LEGENDS CUSTOMS sign. Layout: our own mockup. A lift post between the camera and the car fades.
- **Parts:** 47 slots, 7 categories, 150+ parts and 43 engine swaps. One part per slot. Old saves: Street / Sport / Race became Uncommon / Rare / Epic in the same place (`PartData.OldParts`, exhaust -> mid-pipe so no car gets louder by itself), installed. Test: every car with 12 random old sets, none worse.
- **Prices:** list price x 0.1 x the car's factor ((price / $50K) ^ 0.5, 1 to 10): the icon sheets' numbers are what the dearest cars pay, and on a cheap car they come out at the old Street / Sport / Race prices. Swaps: the prompt's list x the factor. Tune after play-testing.
- **Moving parts:** taking a part off puts it in My Parts. A spare remembers the factor it was paid at, so a dearer car pays the difference (else every part would be bought on the cheapest car). Selling gives 50% of what it was paid. Drops come fully paid.
- **Rare drops:** daily streak 20%, race gold 10%, bank job 25% (`Config.CarParts.Drops`); wheels: Rare 1.5% on SPIN, Epic 2% and Legendary 0.4% on SUPER SPIN, in the ODDS.
- **Swaps:** power climbs from the engine's stock to its tuned max as the power slots fill. 0-60 follows power and weight, with a floor by drive (RWD 2.6 s). Own rev limit and layout sound. Fits every petrol car but stock S2 / R / X.
- **Sound parts:** Pop, Crackle, Burble, Anti-Lag ECUs, muffler parts, decat, downpipe, turbo whistle, blow-off, on the sounds already in the game (`Config.Sounds` Shared*). Road test: 9 pops with distortion on one lift-off, whistle on. **Engine sounds:** only one shared loop and one high-revving recording exist, so a swap's layout is a pitch of those (`LAYOUT` in `Audio`). The owner's ids would make them real.
- **Nitrous:** a fitted bottle turns on the old nitro key and HUD, refills slowly, off in Mountain Road (`Config.Activities.NoNitrous`).
- **Brand check:** web searches found no car-parts company with any of the 8 part brand names. The 43 engines stay as listed.
- **Not tested:** a real phone (phone size in Studio yes), 2 players, opening from the garage and Legends Customs (same call as the menu, which was tested).

The Golf isn't used (owner). Pictures: [[Starter pick - new cars.jpg]], [[Focus C346 - before and after.jpg]], [[Focus C346 - driver seat.jpg]].

No Ford oval or lettering: badges flat in the paint, chrome letters cut, a "C346" plate over the moulded oval on the steering hub (`HUB_PLATES` in `CarSkin`). Changed a little: own wheels, oval tips, diffuser fins, spoiler end plates, tail lamp inside, grille mesh, "C346" script on the boot (`tools/focus_fix.py`). Live needles, digits; brake lamps.
- **Owner, 2026-11-06:** "the whole car angle is off". The body was turned 8.68 degrees on the car. The model sat at yaw -98.68 in `CarModels`, and when a model has no seat or PrimaryPart and its FL / FR corners are Models, not parts, `CarSkin` falls back to a straight world axis. Every other car sits at a multiple of 90. Fixed by turning the model to 0: **after inserting a car, check its pivot's rotation.**
- **Grey / white parts cause:** the FBX brought its own SurfaceAppearances as well as ours, one with an emissive id that doesn't exist (`rbxassetid://51`). Deleted them; ours (`Pbr`) stay, and the cabin got its surfaces back.

## FPS (2026-11-07, owner: "Optimize the game for better FPS")
Measured on the Studio PC only: phones can't be measured from here.
- **Numbers (fps, parked, 4 places):** marina 103, customs 90, highway 138, Legends Rewards 98. Render CPU 8-10 ms, GPU 7-8 ms, scripts about 1.8 ms a frame.
- **What costs most:** shadows (120 fps with them off, 100 with). Then traffic (108 with none). Post effects, atmosphere, clouds, pedestrians, own car: 2 fps or less each. Hiding 16K far small parts: nothing.
- **Done:** Medium graphics has no shadows (phones); challenge chips only within 240 studs; far traffic (over 380 studs) thinks every 3rd frame; penthouse party check twice a second; 2,083 map meshes from Precise to Automatic render fidelity (no gain measured on the PC; it lets phones use simpler far meshes, and it can't be set back part by part).
- **After the new cars (2026-11-07, parked, GLS, the heaviest):** marina 116, customs 96, highway 150, Legends Rewards 92 fps: not worse.
- **Tried and dropped:** streaming radius 480 instead of 640: fewer parts but *lower* FPS (marina 92, customs 66). Stays 640.

## Racing Hub (2026-11-06, owner: "collect all of those racing areas and make a racing hub at the Old Rock place")
All 5 race rings are on the Old Rock's palace square. Pictures: [[Racing Hub - arch.jpg]], [[Racing Hub - palace square.jpg]].

## Drives, surfaces, bends (2026-11-06, day, owner's pictures)
Owner: "I told you to fix these ... Find all buildings and roads if they have this problem", "its happening on this too and also on some buildings, FIND IT ALL", "make all the roads smooth like in the blender but mainly the turns".

**The drive in the picture (Legends Rewards):** `EntranceRamp` is now one straight plane from the lot's edge to the road's edge. It was up to six slabs over the ground's outline: it measured the pavement it was about to remove, which bent it, gave the lines across and the curved dark edges, and dropped the last stud at up to 37 degrees.
- Kerb and pavement pieces half in the drive are cut back to its edge. Left whole they came up through the slab in strips.
- Edges are the road's own kerb stone, flush with the slab.
- 57 drives: all one slab, none over 11 degrees (19 were steeper). Two lots stood too close to the road: the slab starts inside the lot, in a notch cut from the plate (`opts.notch`: Rewards east drive, Legends Motors). The Rewards setts now run to the ramp.
- Drove the Rewards drive both ways at 25-40 studs a second: no jump. Picture: [[Legends Rewards drive - before and after.jpg]].

**"Find it all", the walls:** new `tools/riviera/SurfaceCheck.luau` looks at every flat face on the map for two faces in one place. Cause on the buildings: walls are piers and floor bands that overlap. They had been pushed apart by tiny, uneven amounts, so over 110,000 pairs sat under 0.05 studs apart and showed patches of each other's plaster.
- Buildings: bands on one plane, piers 0.08 behind. Under 100 pairs left (a piece's 0.6-wide end at a corner doesn't count).
- New `src/server/SurfaceFix.lua` does the rest: each group of fighting faces gets planes 0.08 apart. Old Rock, Stadium, Dressing, Sites, Roads done in the place. Venues are built by code, so it runs on the server 8 seconds after start.
- Venues: thousands of pairs down to about 250, small or between tilted pieces.
- **Not proved:** the owner's second picture (a wall with two tones) wasn't found by position. It matches the overlapping wall pieces, but check it in game.
- Undo: `SurfaceFix.undo(root)` (`OldFixCFrame`).

**"Find it all", the roads:** where two roads met, each one's pavement ran on over the other's. New `tools/riviera/PaveOverlap.luau`: the longer road's pavement runs through. 80 pieces and 68 stray kerbs removed, about 400 lowered: 136 level overlaps down to 0. Backup: `ServerStorage.PaveOverlap_2026-11-06`, `PaveOverlap.undo(roads)`.

**Smooth bends:** new `tools/riviera/SmoothKerbs.luau`. The builder lays one piece per road sample, up to 30 studs long on a tight bend, so bends were chains of straight bits. Every stretch with a kink over 1.5 degrees is redrawn along a curve in 2.5-stud kerbs and 4-stud pavements: 552 stretches, 5,457 pieces replaced by 13,884. Red-and-white stripes kept. Picture: [[Smooth kerbs - bends.jpg]]. Backup: `ServerStorage.OldKerbs_2026-11-06`, `SmoothKerbs.undo(roads)`.
- `tools/riviera/PaveDig.luau`: dug the ground off 191 pavement pieces it stood over.
- Lane check after it all: 188 lanes, 0 holes, 0 kerbs in a lane, 0 ledges, 163 speed bumps.

**Checked in Play after it all (Studio open):** the tight bend at the top of the Switchbacks is a clean curve. FPS with the HUD on: Coast Road driving 148, Corniche Highway driving 150. Rewards parked 163, Harbour parked 145 (HUD off). 0 to 2 slow frames. These aren't comparable with last night's numbers: the spots and the PC's load differ. They do show the extra parts cost nothing that matters.

**Not done:**
- Before / after of the worst 10 curves: the "before" pieces are in the backup, so only "after" shots can be taken without undoing the work.
- A thin dark crack across the asphalt at that bend (a gap between two road triangles).
- `world_test` stops in `GemShowcase` (`part.Position` in Lune). Not from these changes.

## Real road meshes (2026-11-06, owner: "make all the roads smooth like in the blender")
Every road on the Riviera is now shown by real curved meshes, made in Blender and uploaded through Open Cloud to the group. 675 meshes, 432,000 triangles, in `Workspace.Riviera.RoadMeshes`. Picture: [[Road meshes - bends.jpg]].
- **Road surface:** the place's own 50,000 triangles, joined up, so hand fixes are kept and the hairline cracks between triangles are gone.
- **Kerbs:** a rounded profile along a smooth curve. **Pavements and shoulders:** one continuous ribbon, so no lines between pieces. Red-and-white stripes kept. Long white lines redrawn along the curve.
- **Islands between roads (owner's picture: "the curb is too wide and not good"):** 14 rebuilt from their outline: rounded corners, no thin tips, a normal kerb all round.
- **Ground through the pavement:** rewritten under 109 pieces. The patches on the bends are gone in the shots.
- **Drives:** cut out of the kerbs and pavements. Pavement stubs lying on the asphalt at junctions left out.

**What stays:** the old road, kerb, pavement, line and island parts are still in the place, invisible, with no change to collision: cars, traffic, the lane test and every raycast use them exactly as before. Only the look changed.

**Checked in Play:** the Switchbacks bend, the island, the Rewards drive, a junction. No new script errors. FPS: in one run, meshes 52 and old parts 51, so no gain and no loss. Part of that run's low numbers was the PC (Studio at 9 GB after many test runs); 73-79 driving, against 148-150 earlier the same day, was not traced further. The FPS gain people expected needs the old parts removed, and they are the collision.

**Not done:** a phone test, the worst-10 before / after set. The white edge line by that island still has one soft kink. Serpent Road, Summit Pass and the Grand Hairpin's own trim stay as they were, except the road surface and lines.

**If a road changes:** the meshes don't follow. Export again and run `tools/road_mesh/` (see `Games/CityLegends/CLAUDE.md`).

**Undo:** `RoadMeshes.undo(roads)` (`tools/riviera/RoadMeshes.luau`): shows the old parts and removes the meshes.

## Overnight run, 2026-11-06 (`Prompts/02 Overnight run (cars, smoothness, roads, buildings, gates, morning fog).md`)
**Choices made overnight** (each easy to undo):
- **Streaming:** `Workspace.StreamingTargetRadius` 1024 -> 640, `StreamOutBehavior` Opportunistic. Why: the stutter at speed was the map streaming in, not scripts. Undo: set them back in Workspace's properties.
- **Road pieces:** `CastShadow` off on all `Road` wedges (14,330), collision off on `RailPost` (3,276; the rails still collide). Undo: a loop in Studio.
- **Graphics:** Low turns off shadows and terrain grass; grass only on High and Ultra; a phone with no saved choice starts on Medium (`WorldFx.setQuality`, `defaultQuality`).
- **Carbon:** one sharp 512 px twill (`tools/lambo/carbon_tile.py`, same weave scale as the old 128 px tile) on the 14 Forza-file cars' carbon parts; each part keeps `OldCarbonMap`. SVJ, SVJ 63, Veyron and Chiron kept theirs: their carbon pictures aren't the same tile, so the scale could be wrong.

**Numbers, step 2** (Studio, PC, Ultra; a run along a lane at speed, 14 s; frames over 33 ms):
| Run | Before | After |
|---|---|---|
| Coast Road, 120 studs/s | 31 fps, max 489 ms, 86 frames | 56 fps, max 77 ms, 44 frames |
| Corniche Highway, 140 studs/s | 65 fps, max 131 ms, 55 frames | 87 fps, max 42 ms, 3 frames |
| Parked: The Harbour / Legends Rewards | 64 / 57 fps, max 20 / 28 ms | |

- Scripts were never the problem: about 3 ms a frame in all (`ScriptProfilerService`), the top ones Traffic 0.6, Main 0.6, WorldFx LOD 0.6. Client parts: 37k-115k now, 158k-234k before (they piled up without Opportunistic). 347 lights, 6,300 shadow casters.
- Not done: Roads are 183k parts (97k wedges). Meshes would cut that hard but need an Import 3D by the owner.
- Not measured: phone size, 2 players (the MCP drives one client).
- **Parked, after:** The Harbour 87 fps (was 64), Legends Rewards 83 (was 57), Grand Hairpin Hotel 134. No frame over 33 ms at any of the three.
- **Graphics levels at The Harbour:** Ultra 93 fps, High 93, Medium 95, Low 103. Low also draws fewer far parts (LOD scale), which this test doesn't show. Radius 640 doesn't show gaps: from the mountain road the far towers and stand-ins are there.
- **Car and camera:** the car's place on screen moves by 0.003-0.004 studs from frame to frame (no jitter between car and camera). Nothing to fix there. The car's position only changes at physics rate (60 a second): at 120+ fps the whole view steps at 60, as in any Roblox game.

**Step 4: time and cold morning (done):**
- **`Config.DayCycle.CycleMinutes` 14** (was 24): about 35 s a game hour, about 3.5 minutes of full night plus 2 of dusk and dawn.
- **`DayCycle.lua`, Riviera keys:** new key at 7:12 (the thickest mist); 6:00, 8:30, 13:00 and 16:30 are cooler, softer and paler; contrast below zero until midday. New key fields `soft` (`Lighting.ShadowSoftness`) and `cover` (cloud cover). Mist = `density` + `haze` in the keys: density 0.33 at 7:12, 0.25 at 13:00, 0.22 from 16:30. To change it, edit those numbers.
- **Sunset and night were too dark (seen in the shots):** at 18:18 the road was near black, at night the buildings were black shapes. `ambient`, `outdoor`, `exposure` raised on the 18:18, 19:36 and night keys, contrast lowered on the first two. Sun and lamps are the same.
- **Blue bars at night:** the "TV glow" window colour landed on whole balcony bands, which read as blue neon. Now only on small windows (`WorldFx.setWindow`).
- Pictures: `Day cycle - cold morning, sunset, night.jpg`, `Day cycle - mist on the mountain.jpg`.

**Step 5: buildings (done):**

- **After:** 1 pair (0.1 studs, between two pavements), 0 parts in a neighbour. 257 buildings moved, nearly all under 2 studs, 2 of them 5-9 studs; the far stand-ins moved with them. Undo: `OldPivot` attribute on each moved model, `Unoverlap.undo()`.
- **Trimmed:** 26 balcony and trim pieces that stood in a neighbour's wall: `ServerStorage.TrimmedParts_2026-11-06`. **Removed:** 1 building at x 14092, z 256, behind the row (no room): `ServerStorage.RemovedBuildings_2026-11-06`, with its stand-in.

(2) A lamp streamed in by day stayed lit: `WorldFx` now sets it on arrival. (3) The Toolbox farmhouse carried 120 ceiling spots and its front burned white at night: one in three stays, at 60%.

## Features so far
- **City:** a big night city with traffic that stops at red lights, and a tunnel intro cutscene ("CITY LEGENDS").
- **Driving and money:** you earn money by driving and by cutting up traffic, with combos. You buy cars in the garage.
- **Cars:** 55 cars in 3 tiers (Starter, Mid, High), remade 2026-10-10 from the owner's list of real cars. Names are close look-alikes (e.g. "BNW M3 F80", "Toyoka Land Cruzer 200", "Lambrini Urus"). Free starter: Suzaki Swiff (`suzaki_swift`).
- **HUD:** a Highway Legends-style HUD with level/XP bar, rev counter, cash, side buttons and a daily gift.
- **Weather:** a rain cycle with wet roads, plus blue exhaust flames. Rain is rare since 2026-11-03 (owner: "its raining too much, make the rain rarer"): you join in clear weather, dry spells last 20-40 minutes, showers 3-5 minutes (was 4-8 minutes each, starting wet).
- **Sounds and lighting:** a full soundscape and night lighting.

## Decisions
- Car names stay fictional but close enough to recognise (no real brands or badges).
- **New brand names (2026-10-31, owner):** every make got a new name; the model part of each name stays for now (owner will rename models later). BMW = Bavarica Werkz, Mercedes = Tri-Star Silber, Brabus = Rocket, Toyota = Apex, Lexus = Stealth-Apex, Nissan = Horizon, Mazda = Ahura, Suzuki = Sompact, Kia = Orient, BYD = Dream-Build Volt, Xiaomi = HyperSpeed Tech, Chevrolet = Golden, Dodge = Hemi, Porsche = Stuttgart, Audi = Precision, Aston Martin = British Vanguard, Ferrari = Rosso Rampante, Lamborghini = Enfurecido, Maserati = Trident Motors, McLaren = Speedmark, Bentley = Sovereign, Rolls-Royce = Ecstasy, Bugatti = Velocité Noir. Saved for cars not in the game yet: Mitsubishi = Ralliart, Range Rover = Titan Realm, Pagani = Carbonio Maestro, Koenigsegg = Valhalla. Some are real trademarks (Ralliart, Hemi, Rocket, Valhalla): owner knows and will rename later. Car ids (`bnw_...`) stay the same, so saves are safe; brand XP moves to the new names by itself.
- The cars Claude generated from code didn't look good enough. Plan: use real car models from the Creator Store, inserted by hand in Studio. Delete their scripts and put them in `ReplicatedStorage/CarModels`, named after the car id.
- No automatic downloading of Creator Store models by the game.
- Money can be set from the server console: `game.Players.NAME:SetAttribute("SetCash", amount)` (or `"AddCash"`). Use the Studio command bar in Server mode, or the F9 server console.
- Testers can own every car from the same server console: `game.Players.NAME:SetAttribute("UnlockAllCars", true)` (2026-10-22).
- Unlock everything (2026-10-23, owner): `game.Players.NAME:SetAttribute("UnlockEverything", true)` gives every car, the level every car needs (20), every penthouse and hotel suite, both island house types, every chauffeur contract, the Black VIP card and every flex item. Cash stays as it is (use SetCash).
- Driver hands (2026-10-22): in the in-car camera the forearms and hands stay on the wheel (only the part near the camera is cut off); if the avatar can't load, plain default arms are used.
- Claude sends the game as a place file (`CityLegends.rbxlx`) after each change.
- Never share Roblox or alt account logins or cookies with Claude (offered once, declined).
- Car looks can be inspired by real cars, but names and badges stay fictional.
- One big island (2026-10-29, owner): the three islands become one big island with a mountain and a beach, real roads (no grass roads), and house tiers by height: Low at the bottom near the bridge, Mid halfway up, High on top in a police-gated community. Plus a real estate office with 25+ house designs (1-story to mansions). Prompt for Claude Code: `Prompts/One big island and real estate.md`.
- Neon only at sunset and night (2026-10-29, owner): city neon signs and strips are off by day, fade on from sunset, full at night; traffic, car and police lights unchanged. Plus more realistic lighting and environment. Prompt: `Prompts/Neon at night and realistic environment.md`.
- Camera and car movement like the owner's reference video (2026-10-29, `Games/CityLegends/imports/Cars FOV and Driving physcis.mp4`, a racing-game mod, STOCK vs ACTIVE): calmer chase FOV (speed feel from lag, distance, sway, shake and blur instead), head lean, braking dive and breathing in the cockpit (cabin FOV stays fixed), high-speed sway, body roll / squat / dive, camera settings. Prompt: `Prompts/Camera and car movement like the video.md`.
- Houses: buy at the showroom, then place (2026-10-29, owner): no pre-placed houses; Legends Realty showroom with 30+ houses from $150K (smallest) to $100M (biggest); after buying, the player picks a FOR SALE lot of that tier (Low bottom, Mid middle, High top). High tier lots spread over the mountain top with private driveways (reference photo `Games/CityLegends/imports/refs/houses/high_tier_hilltop.webp`), not in a row. Gate at the High tier entrance. Smooth island roads with matching lane lines. Prompt: `Prompts/Houses - buy at showroom, place on a lot.md`.
- The 5 most expensive houses get a 10-car garage plus 3 cars on the driveway (owner, 2026-10-29); every house has its own small gate.
- Housing like Driving Empire's (2026-10-29, owner): the system works like Driving Empire (Houses Dealership blueprint shop, claim a plot of the right size, one placed house at a time, free garage spaces plus more bought with cash, usable interiors), but our own UI (fonts, layout, sizes); the houses look close to Driving Empire's but not identical, with our own names. Prompt: `Prompts/Housing like Driving Empire.md`.
- Dealership menu like Midnight Racing's (2026-10-29, owner): counter + E opens a menu with brands along the bottom, the car list on the left, stats on the right, name / cost / BUY / EXIT on top; each car has trims from worst to best and most trims need levels (references in `Games/CityLegends/imports/refs/dealership/`). Brand tiles are text only (no emblems). Owner chose (B): cars from $600K are no longer market-only; every car and trim is bought at the dealer once you reach its level (top cars at high levels), and the car market stays as a second way; no need to own the previous trim. Prompt: `Prompts/Dealership with trims like Midnight Racing.md`.
- Research the top driving games (2026-10-30, owner): Claude Code studies 10 Roblox driving games (wiki, videos, and playing only if computer use is on and the owner agrees), saves the lessons in `City Legends/Research - Driving games.md` (read before planning any feature), then proposes ranked improvements. Prompt: `Prompts/Research 10 driving games.md`.
- New map shaped like Monaco (2026-10-30, owner: the current map looks bad): a long coastal strip with two harbours, a headland old town, curvy hill roads, hairpins and a tunnel; fictional names only; built in a copy `CityLegends_Monaco.rbxl` in phases, and only replaces the old map when the owner says so. Needs a curved road system instead of the grid. Reference `Games/CityLegends/imports/refs/map/monaco_shape.webp`. Prompt: `Prompts/New map shaped like Monaco.md`.
- **Imported Monaco map + low-poly traffic cars (2026-11-01, owner):** the owner will send a ready-made Monaco map and new low-poly car models for the traffic only (player cars stay as they are). Plan: take the imported map and make it replace the current city (traffic lanes, spawns, venues and minimap move to the new roads). Files arrived 2026-11-01 (`imports/asphalt_8_-_monacoazure_coast.glb`, `imports/traffic_rider_npc_vehicles.glb`) but NOT used: both are rips of other companies' games (Asphalt 8 by Gameloft, Traffic Rider), uploaded to Sketchfab by people who don't own them; the cars are also non-commercial only and real brands (BMW, Chevrolet, Ford...). The map is also a race track, not a drivable city. Waiting for the owner to choose another way.
- **Monaco map wishes (2026-11-01, owner):** the old City Legends map looks too blocky; the new one must look realistic: real terrain (hills, cliffs, beach), smooth curving roads, not boxes on a grid. A big loop round the mountain with **3 different routes** round the city so players don't get bored. **No yachts** in the bay. Owner first asked to turn the Asphalt 8 track into a city; Claude explained a changed copy is still Gameloft's, and offered to build it from Monaco's real shape (OpenStreetMap) instead. Traffic cars: owner will sort them out and say later.
- **Riviera map, Phase 1 plan (2026-11-01):** 2D plan made, waiting for the owner's OK before any 3D: ![[Riviera map - Phase 1 plan.png]] Built from Monaco's real coastline, roads (OpenStreetMap) and heights, at 1.6 studs per metre across and 1 stud per metre up; about 7,000 x 3,000 studs. Three long routes (Coast Road along the harbour with a tunnel, Town Road through the middle, Mountain Road / Summit Road high up with viaducts and two short tunnels), joined at both ends by hairpin switchbacks into one big loop; cross links: Rock Climb, Ravine Climb, Casino Climb. Districts: Riviera Bay (new-build, stadium), Old Rock (palace square loop), Harbour Quarter, Casino Heights (casino, hotel, gem showcase), Hillside Terraces west and east (houses), Sandy Point (beach). Harbours: quays and breakwaters only, no yachts. Steepest: 8% on the 3 routes, 10% on climbs. Tools and data: `Games/CityLegends/tools/map_plan/`.
- **Plan v2 (2026-11-01, owner: "add more roads and more city blocks so players can cruise", "make the map very very realistic"; owner will import Monaco buildings and realistic trees):** about 50 roads now (~53,000 studs of road, 66 junctions): West Town (Upper Street + 6 diagonal climbs), Harbour Quarter (Harbour Street, Ravine Street, 2 climbs), Casino Heights / east town (Casino Avenue, Beach Avenue, 4 climbs), Riviera Bay loop with a cross, Hillside Terraces lanes with hairpins up to Summit Road. Town climbs up to 14% (Monaco is steep), the 3 routes 8%. Still waiting for the owner's OK before 3D.
- **Riviera map, Phase 1 terrain (2026-11-01, owner: "OK. Start map plan V2", videos of Monaco at night and morning coming):** built in the same place, far east of the old city (plan (0,0) at X 14000, Z 0; the old map stays as it is until the owner says switch). Real ground from a dense 25 m height grid (EU-DEM), the OpenStreetMap coastline, harbours with quays and breakwaters (no yachts), beaches, about 1 km of hills round it and 2 km of open sea; all ~50 roads cut in as smooth asphalt with pavements, banks and cuttings, the coast tunnel carved; the mountains behind at their real steepness, the town gentler (0.75 studs a metre up) so streets stay at most 16% steep. Terrain colours changed for the Riviera look (this also tints the old map's terrain; old colours saved on Terrain attribute `OldMaterialColors`). Pictures: ![[Riviera terrain - first pass.jpg]] ![[Riviera terrain - top view.png]]. Next: proper road surfaces (lane lines, kerbs), buildings on the blocks, trees and palms, street lamps, the warm night look from the owner's videos.
- **Riviera harbour district + whole-map first pass (night of 2026-11-01, owner asleep: "continue all night, do everything without my permission"):** all ~36 roads built as parts in Studio (Workspace.Riviera): a seamless triangle-ribbon carriageway (no steps between pieces; owner: "no bumps or extra studs"), red-pink pavements with granite kerbs, white lines, zebra crossings; junctions made flat and side roads levelled onto them; a bump checker drives every lane a stud at a time. Main roads are clean (Coast Road 0 steps, Town Road 1); some tight junction corners on the side streets still need work (about 360 spots over 0.3 studs on the whole map, most on inner lanes of tight bends). Harbour front lowered to just above the quays; white stone balustrade and umbrella pines along the harbour; the coast tunnel with round white pillars open to the sea, sodium lights and walkways; 692 street lamps (warm sodium at night); 588 buildings (1970s apartment blocks with long balconies, Belle Epoque in cream / ochre / peach / pink with shutters, iron balconies, cornice and slate mansard; Casino Heights mostly Belle Epoque); stone retaining walls where the hill stands above the pavement; bollards, blue crossing signs, rows of parked scooters, planters with bougainvillea, palms (the imported palm), bus shelters. Riviera light (DayCycle): clear deep-blue day, gold sunset, navy blue hour, warm night; about 4 in 10 windows glow warm at night (WorldFx, tag Window). Pictures in this folder: "Riviera harbour road - video vs game", "Riviera tunnel - video vs game", "Riviera apartment street - video vs game", "Riviera city - from the sea", "Riviera casino street", "Riviera harbour road - night", "Riviera city - night from the sea". About 179,000 parts in the Riviera so far (heavy: needs a performance pass before it goes live). Not yet: Old Rock palace and old town, Riviera Bay stadium, the casino square, hillside villas, Summit Road barriers and real viaducts, traffic and spawns on the new map. **The owner must save the place (Ctrl+S) to keep it**; everything can also be rebuilt from the repo tools (Games/CityLegends/tools/riviera/*.luau, map_plan/*.py).

- **Riviera speed pass + low-graphics check (2026-11-02, owner: "continue what you need to do"):**
  - Shadows off for everything flat or hidden: 64k road pieces (road, lines, pavement, kerbs, zebras) and 49k building skin pieces (the dark core behind the skin still casts the building's shadow). Small building detail now hides past 650-700 studs (was 800-900). Code: `Roads.luau` newPart, `Buildings.luau` quiet().
  - Studio PC FPS at the casino street, noon: 49 before -> 85 after; night 81; on graphics level 3: 156.
  - **Low graphics:** Roblox itself shortens the view distance on low and medium graphics (most phones). On level 3 the summit shows no city at all; on level 6 only the nearest ~1000 studs. The far city lights show fully only on high graphics. Pictures: `Riviera summit - night on low graphics (level 3)`, `... medium graphics (level 6)`. Fixed (same day, owner: "do both"): Roblox still draws billboards past that distance, so `FarCity` adds one billboard of lit-window dots per building (folder `RivieraFar.Lights`, tag FarLight; inside the building, so hidden on high graphics). WorldFx switches them on only at night, past 450 studs, in view, with the camera above 110 (hills) and when the player's graphics setting is not 8+; costs ~2.5 ms a frame while on. Studio preview: workspace attribute `PreviewFarLights`. Picture: `Riviera summit - night on low graphics, with far lights (after)`.
  - Only each building's core collides and answers rays (`Buildings.luau`); all the rest is just for show.
  - **Meshes instead of parts (owner asked):** not done, on purpose. Roblox draws thousands of plain boxes very cheaply (it batches them); 664 unique building meshes would each be drawn on their own, and they'd have to be uploaded from your account. What actually cost FPS was shadows, fixed above.
- **Old Rock: palace square and old town (2026-11-02, owner: "do both"):** new `tools/riviera/OldRock.luau` (Workspace.Riviera.OldRock) on the rock inside the Old Rock loop. Fictional names: the **Rock Palace** and **St Aurel Cathedral**.
  - Palace square: cobbles at height 52, six lanterns (lit at night), benches, four cannons with shot piles; a ramp up from the north side of the loop so cars can drive up.
  - Rock Palace (west side, facing the square): three ochre floors with stone window frames, battlements, a crenellated corner tower with a red and white flag, the great gate with a balcony, two red and white sentry boxes.
  - St Aurel Cathedral (south of the square): white stone, gable front with three portals and a rose window, two bell towers, a dome, the round apse, steps.
  - Old town: 32 narrow 4-5 storey houses (the buildings kit) packed along cobbled lanes east, south and north of the square. The 21 ordinary buildings that stood inside the loop are gone.
  - Far city rebuilt with the Old Rock in it. About 4,300 parts. Pictures: `Riviera Old Rock - palace square, day`, `... night`, `Riviera Old Rock - old town from above`.
  - **Finished (same day, owner: "finish Old rock"):** `OldRock.finish`: 4 palace guards in white (white cap, red sash, rifle) by the sentry boxes and the cathedral doors; 123 crenellated stone rampart pieces along the rock edge just inside the loop road wherever nothing stands; gardens of grass on the free ground with 45 umbrella pines and palms and some hedges. Picture: `Riviera Old Rock - gardens and ramparts`.

- **Road joints and the driver's hands (2026-11-02, owner: "There is a massive bump there", "the road isnt aligned properly here. most of the roads arent aligned properly", "when a player exit a car the hands get left behind"):**
  - **Why the steps:** where a side road met a bigger road, the map data had the side road a stud or two off the bigger road's surface just past its edge, so the two slabs met with a ledge (often right at the zebra crossing). Also bend fans that started next to a junction, and a few roads that curl back over themselves.
  - **Fix (`tools/riviera/Roads.luau`):** before building, every road is eased onto the bigger road's surface at each junction mouth (the samples within its reach take its height, the change fades out along the road over 40+ studs), biggest roads first; a road curling back over itself takes its later part's height where they overlap; edge points up to 8 studs past a bigger road's edge take its height; the free edge points then ease in (no more than 19% along an edge, 6% across); bend pivots near a junction keep the junction's height.
  - **Measured on the whole map** (surface scanned every 1.5 studs round all 66 junctions; every lane driven a stud at a time): junction steps over 0.35 studs 819 -> 249 (junctions with any: 44 -> 26); lane bumps over 0.3 studs 108 -> 97. Left: roads running side by side behind a wall (Ravine Street / Town Road), a few hairpins (East Climb 3 and 4, Rock Climb, the West Switchbacks end).
  - Lamps, scooters and furniture re-seated on the new pavement heights.
  - **Hands left behind:** the driver's arms are a local copy of the avatar's arms posed on the wheel, and the steering wheel and gauge needles are unwelded and posed by hand while driving. Getting out left them all where they were. Now `Driving.detach` removes the arms and puts the wheel and needles back at rest, welded to the car. Not tested by a real exit yet.
- **Car over studs and bumps (2026-11-02, owner: "I dont think the roads are the problem. It might be the car, The car is jerking and jump and lifting up too much when there is a stud or a bump"):** in `Driving.lua` / `Config.DrivingFeel`:
  - the bump kick fired on any 0.18-stud change under the car with a random sideways roll: now only past 0.45 studs, about a third as strong, no random roll (`BumpKick` 0.5 -> 0.18);
  - the springs' mean travel is smoothed and the body rises to a bump slower than it settles (`LiftEase` 5, `SettleEase` 12): a stud under one wheel barely lifts the body, the wheels still move;
  - the road plane under the four wheels: a sudden tilt over ~6 degrees (one wheel on a stud) eases in slowly; real slope changes still come through;
  - the collision hull sits higher (`HullLift` 0.9 -> 1.3), so a small step slides under it instead of hitting it and shoving the car up.
  - Checked: the game starts and a car spawns with no errors. Not driven (Studio test input doesn't reach the car), so the feel is untested.

  - **Switch:** `Config.World.OldCity = false` (`Config.monacoOnly()`): the old city, Downtown, the west and Legends Isle aren't built; CityBuilder makes an empty `City` folder and builds only the venues (all at their Monaco sites).
  - **Old map kept, unseen:** Workspace.City (the baked map, ~169k parts) plus the owner's imported "Car Dealership" and "Sketchfab_Scene" models moved to `ServerStorage.OldMap` (players never load it). The old map's terrain (x -16,000 to 8,400) was cleared; it can be made again by the scenery build in code (`CityBuilder.build("scenery")`), and the owner's `CityLegends.rbxl` backup still has everything. To bring the old map back: OldCity = true and move City back to Workspace.
  - **Moved to Monaco** (`tools/map_plan/export_world.py` -> `src/shared/RivieraWorld.lua`):
    - **Houses:** 47 lots on the hillside lanes kept free of buildings: 22 Low on Terrace W1, 13 Mid on Terrace E2 / Hairpin W3, 12 High along the Mountain Road over the city; each faces its lane behind the pavement, cleared and levelled (`Sites.luau`). Lot ids are new (`riv_low_1` ...): houses placed on the old island lots need placing again.
    - **Legends Realty** (the house catalogue pavilion) on its own lot by the harbour on the Coast Road; no island booth; the Summit gate is off (Monaco's top lots are open).
    - **Races:** Coast Road Run, Mountain Road, Town Road Sprint, Casino Avenue (a checkpoint about every 180 studs). **Deliveries:** Harbour Dispatch and Casino Dispatch, 12 drop points round town. **Legends chips:** 25 on Monaco's pavements.
    - **Speed cameras:** 50 along the Coast, Town and Mountain Roads (were the old city's 60).
    - **Chauffeur:** places are the Monaco venues plus Old Rock Palace, Riviera Bay Stadium, The Harbour and the Mountain Road lookout; the old city's stands are off.
    - Traffic and police only on Monaco's lanes; the minimap draws only Monaco; speed traps only Monaco's; jump ramps off.
  - **Not moved:** the jump ramps, the towers' underground garages (and the parked cars to steal in them), the beach / island scenery.
  - Checked in Play: all venues at their Monaco sites, 47 lots with signs on level pads (picture `Monaco house lots - Terrace W1`), 32 cars all in Monaco, 50 cameras in Monaco, start-up clean. Client memory in Studio 8.9 GB -> 6.5 GB. Not played through (buying a house, a race, a delivery).
- **Grainy look, flickering kerbs and roads (2026-11-03, owner: "the game is looking a little bit grainy", "its also from the curbs and sometimes the roads are glitching out", whole screen):**
  - No film-grain filter was on. Causes found: (1) the motion blur (one Roblox Blur over the whole screen) went up to about 4-7 at speed, 3 on every gear change, 6 on a launch: it is now about a third of that (`Config.Camera` Immersive Blur 4 from 50% of the rush, Low 6 from 40%, chase up to ~0.6; ShiftBlur 1, LaunchBlur 2, NitroBlur 1.5). Settings > Motion blur Off still turns it off. (2) Kerbs were Granite, a strongly speckled texture: all 12,179 Riviera kerbs are now Concrete. (3) Kerbs flickered: each kerb's road-side face sat exactly on the pavement's and the road's edge, and its top only 0.04 above the pavers. Every kerb now stands 0.05 nearer the road and 0.06 higher (attribute `KerbNudged`). (4) Roads flickered at junctions: where a side road meets a bigger road their triangles lay in the same plane (1,088 spots). The side road's overlapping triangles were lowered 0.06 (a few 0.03-0.2) under the bigger road (attribute `JunctionLowered`, 860 pieces); about 20 small spots left. (5) Lane lines and zebra stripes less than 0.06 above the road (some just under it) flickered: 2,345 raised to 0.08 above it (attribute `PaintRaised`).
  - **Kerbs still flickering (owner, same day: "some curbs are still glitching out"):** kerb and pavement pieces along a street overlap their neighbours, and the pieces were only 0.006 apart in height, so the overlaps flickered (about 4,500 spots). Every second piece (the slightly narrower ones) is now 0.045 higher (attribute `AltRaised`, 12,162 pieces), and where pieces still met within 0.05 the lower one went down a little more (attribute `OverlapLowered`, ~3,300 moves, at most 0.2). A re-check found about 650 close spots left after the first pass; a second pass moved 500 of them.
  - Done in the open Studio place (map edit) and in `tools/riviera/Roads.luau` (kerb material and position) for a rebuild; the junction and paint fixes are only in the place. **Owner: save the place.**
- **No walkers on the hills, more traffic, map loading screen (2026-11-03, owner: "dont add walking npc near the summits but npc in the busy city area is fine but add more traffic cars throughout the map. also add a loading screen to finsih loading the map so that players dont need to play with loading map and lag"):**
  - Pedestrians walk only in the town: none on Mountain Road, the Hairpins, Terraces or Switchbacks, and no walker path goes above 92 studs up (`Pedestrians.lua`, `TOWN_TOP`). Checked in Play: parked on Terrace W1, the only walkers were on the town streets ~250 studs below.
  - Traffic: 64 cars (was 40), all of them can be in Monaco (`Config.Traffic` CarCount 64, RivieraMax 64). Phones and low graphics get about 60%. Checked: 64 cars, Studio 71 FPS.
  - Loading the map: the loading screen no longer goes after 10 s whatever. It stays up until the map round the player has stopped streaming in (no new parts for 1.5 s, at most 30 s), at the start ("Loading the map..."), when your car comes out and after a fast travel ("Loading the streets...", the car held still meanwhile). SKIP still shows after 5 s (Playbook rule). Checked in Play: start 4 s of map loading, car out 2.6 s (Studio streams fast; real phones will take longer).
- **Road lines on bends and at junctions (2026-11-03, owner: "most of the roads the has a intersection or a wide turn, the road becomes bad with road lines not there properly"):** the lines were laid along straight pieces between centre points and shifted sideways, so on bends they turned the wrong way and crossed each other on the inside (white X marks), and some ran over the other road at junctions. All 23,764 lines were remade by the new `tools/riviera/RoadLines.luau`: each line follows the curve (from one centre point's side spot to the next one's), is left out where the inside of a tight bend folds back, and sits 0.08 above its own road found by raycast; no line runs onto another road's carriageway. 23,310 new lines. Lines more than 15 degrees off their road: 423 before, 224 after (the rest are where two roads merge at a narrow angle and look right). Old lines kept in `ServerStorage.OldRoadLines` for an undo: delete it once you're happy. Zebra crossings unchanged.
- **Speed cameras off, new minimap (2026-11-03, owner: "remove speed cameras for now. make the mini map look way better and smoother"):**
  - Speed cameras: off (`Config.Police.SpeedCameras = false`; true brings all 50 back). Checked in Play: none.
  - Minimap: the map is now a drawn picture (20 tiles, `tools/minimap_art.py` -> `src/client/MinimapArt.lua`, ids in `tools/minimap_art_ids.json`) instead of ~3,000 plain boxes: dark-blue sea with a lighter shore, land shaded by the hills, every building's footprint, roads with pavements and dark edges and round corners, the three long routes in gold. A drawn bevelled rim with a soft inner shadow and a white-and-gold arrow. The map no longer shakes with the camera (its turning is smoothed), and it shows a bit more around you (`Config.Minimap` ZoomNear 0.32, was 0.5). Only the tiles near you load (phones' memory); the full map (click the minimap) uses the same pictures. Checked in Play: it shows the streets and buildings around the car; no FPS cost (35 with or without it in that Studio run).
  - After big map changes, run `python tools/minimap_art.py` again, upload the PNGs and write the module (`--lua`).
- **SVJ cluster under its frame (2026-11-03, owner: "Under the SVJ and SVJ 63 cluster there is the clusters outline cover and can u put the cluster under that outline", red-marker picture):** the SVJ screen now fills both cars' own cluster frame (the middle screen and the side windows) and sits just behind the frame, so the frame's edges and ribs lie over it like the real car. Was: a smaller rectangle over the old screen picture only. Measured on both models in Blender (depth across the frame, `tools/svj_bezel/`): SVJ 0.336 x 0.136 m, SVJ 63 0.324 x 0.132 m. The picture is stretched about 11% taller to fill the frame, and its top corners, which would show past the frame over the windscreen, are cut away (`tools/svj_cluster_mask.png`; new background pictures uploaded, ids in `tools/svj_cluster_ids.json`). Checked in Play from the driver's seat on both cars (daytime): the screen sits inside the hood, its top tucked under the frame's edge. The side windows are mostly behind the steering wheel from the seat. Note: the place has two copies of `SVJClusterArt` in StarterPlayerScripts > Client (both updated).
- **Ground poking through roads (2026-11-03, owner sent a junction where the road was "bugged out"):** at the Coast Road / East Switchbacks junction (about X 17130, Z 770) a levelled terrain pad sat half a stud above the road, so bumpy terrain showed through the asphalt and pavement. A check of every road and pavement piece on the map found 108 spots where terrain stood above the road (junction corners, hairpins, some pavements under banks). The terrain over each piece was cleared down past the slab (attribute `TerrainCleared`, ~120 pieces). 5 left on purpose: West Switchbacks' tunnel (hill above it) and one sample point. Done in the place only (map edit). Owner: save the place.
- **SVJ and SVJ 63 exhaust (2026-11-03, owner sent the ids: start-up, idle, low, mid, high, limiter = the SV's, shift, downshift, shift pops, pops, launch control; "make the layered engine sound system and use these"):** both cars use one set (`Config.Sounds.Exhaust_lambrini_svj_*` / `_svj63_*`). The layered system (`Audio.engine`) now has four loop bands, idle / low / mid / high, each fading into the next round its own revs (equal-power crossfade, pitch follows the revs round the rpm it was recorded at), the limiter loop at the rev limiter, launch control while held, and one-shots: a shift sound on every upshift, a blip on every downshift, shift pops on a hard upshift at high revs, pops when you lift off at high revs (pitch varied a little each time). Cars without a Low band keep the old three-band blend (the LP750). Checked in Studio: all 11 sounds load; a rev sweep gives the right band at each rpm, the limiter at the top, and shifting up and down plays both one-shots. Not heard by Claude (no audio here): the owner should drive one and say if any band is too loud or quiet. Owner (same day): "make the low rev and mid rev louder": low and mid bands now 1.7x (`BAND_GAIN` in `Audio.lua`, four-band cars only).
- **Launch control in manual, Q / E in automatic (2026-11-03, owner: "when cars use manual mode launch control doesnt work ... when using automatic mode when u press E or Q it switches to manual mode"):** launch control (hold brake + throttle at a stop, let go of the brake) now works in manual / sequential too, in a forward gear with the clutch up (dropping the clutch at high revs still launches as well). In automatic, Q / E no longer switch to manual: they only show "Automatic: press M for sequential (Q / E)". M or Settings changes the gearbox. Checked in Play: manual 1st gear, brake + throttle shows "Launch control: let go of the brake", letting go launches (17 studs in 1.5 s); in automatic, E stays automatic and shows the hint.
- **Tunnel sound, smarter traffic, more cars, a less arcade look, speed-ups (2026-11-03, owner: "add a tunnel sound effect if you drive through the tunnel the car becomes 2 times louder", "make the traffic cars smarter, they're bumping into each other at the intersection", "the game look a little bit like an arcade game ... I want it to feel very realistic", "add more traffic cars too", "add more cars on the main city area and optimize the game", "some traffic cars would do a unnecessary U turn or J turn just to turn right or left"):**
  - Tunnel: with a roof over the car here and 25 studs ahead and behind, the engine and exhaust get twice as loud (eased in and out) and the sound gets a stone-corridor echo (`Driving` isInTunnel, `Audio` engine:setTunnel). Checked in Play in the coast tunnel: engine volume 0.52 -> 1.04 with the echo, back to 0.52 outside.
  - Traffic at junctions (`Traffic.lua` crossConflict): every path through a junction knows which other paths cross or merge with it and where (worked out from the curves). A car about to enter waits at the edge while another car is on the crossing spot, or will reach it first (tie: lower number goes). Cars in the junction carry on. A player's car near the junction always has the way. Measured at the Harbour Street junctions over 45 s (same spot, 90 cars): cars overlapping each other 50 -> 15 (crossing at an angle 34 -> 8); cars stand still a little more (18% -> 26% of the time: waiting their turn).
  - U / J turns: a turn that doubles back (over 135 degrees) is only taken at a dead end; sharp turns over 110 degrees are rarer; turn curves now bend round the corner where the way in and the way out meet (`RivieraTraffic`), not a guess that swung wide. Turned right round within 60 studs: 7 -> 3 in the same test.
  - More traffic: 90 cars (was 64), and the hill roads (Mountain Road, hairpins, terraces, switchbacks) get only a quarter of the spots, so the town carries most of it (`Config.Traffic` CarCount 90, RivieraMax 90, HillShare 0.25). Phones ~60%.
  - Look (`DayCycle`, REAL): a photographic grade over the Monaco light: colours a little muted (saturation +0.10 -> -0.06), firmer contrast, deeper shade, a real distance haze (density +0.08, haze at least 0.7), softer bloom, slightly lower brightness.
  - Speed-ups: the road surface (102,777 pieces, 13,578 shadow casters near the player) no longer casts shadows (it lies flat on the ground: no visible change); the old city's monorail (5 lights, moving every frame far away) is off in Monaco-only; the junction check runs only for cars within 500 studs of the camera. Studio FPS with 90 cars: about 62-74.
- **Crossings only in town (2026-11-03, owner: "remove cross walk on wide curves and only put cross walk on the main part of the city map"):** of 284 zebra crossings, 135 were taken out: 57 on the hill roads (Mountain Road, hairpins, terraces, switchbacks) and 78 where the road bends more than 20 degrees over about 70 studs. 149 stay in the town. Their crossing signs and red-white bollards went too (357 pieces; any near a kept crossing stay). Moved, not deleted: `ServerStorage.RemovedCrossings` (drag a street's stripes back to `Workspace.Riviera.Roads.<street>` to restore). `Roads.luau` follows the same rule on a rebuild.
- **Speedometer style setting (2026-11-03, `Prompts/Speedometer style setting (digital or analogue).md`):** new `src/client/HudSpeedo.lua` replaces the old HUD rev counter. Two styles, both with the big italic digital speed (leading zeros dimmed) and the units: **Analogue** (an arc rev dial in thin white lines with a needle, each car's own scale, the redline zone pink-red, the gear in a ring in the middle) and **Digital** (the gear in a box, the speed, a flat segmented rev bar with the redline part pink-red). Both: the redline zone flashes at the limiter, "LC" shows while launch control is held, a pink "M" by the gear in manual, R in amber, electric cars show D on a 16,000 rpm scale. Each car's redline comes from `CarStats.redline` (by class, and the real figure for the cars modelled on known engines: e.g. SVJ 8,700, LFA 9,000, 458 9,000, Rolls 5,500). The needle / bar are eased (no jitter); the number changes only when the value does. Hidden in the in-car view (the car's own cluster) and on foot; the warning lights (blinkers, ENGINE OFF, TC OFF, LIGHTS) sit in a row above it. **Setting:** Main menu > SETTINGS > DRIVING > **Speedometer: ANALOGUE / DIGITAL** (default Analogue), saved per player (`Settings.Speedo`, checked in `PlayerData.setSettings`), switches at once. Studio test hook: workspace attribute `PreviewSpeedo`. Checked in Play (PC): both styles, switching while driving, the shown speed follows the car (72-119 km/h run), gear and M, the dial hidden in the cockpit view, no new errors. Not checked: a phone-size screenshot (no device emulator through the MCP; phones get a smaller 170 px dial / 236 x 92 box, bottom middle as before) and a real rejoin (the setting uses the same save path as the other settings). Picture: [[Speedometer - digital and analogue (reference, ours).jpg]].
- **Speedometer fixes (2026-11-03, prompt "Fixes after the first build"):** both styles have no background box now (the numbers, gear box, dial and bar float on the screen, with a soft dark outline so they read on bright and dark scenes). The "Get out" pill is a slim round pill on the right, above the speedometer and its warning lights, under the minimap's cards (phones: above the right-side steering buttons). Picture: [[Speedometer - no background, Get out pill moved.jpg]]. Not checked at phone size (no emulator through the MCP).
- **Realistic car physics (2026-11-03, `Prompts/Realistic car physics.md`):** measured on a flat test track in Studio (4 cars, automatic, same script before and after; distances in metres at the game's speed scale, 1 stud = 0.35 m; g = real g):

  | Car | 0-100 km/h | Top speed | 100-0 km/h | Corner g at ~100 km/h |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | Sompact Swiff (900 kg) | 10.3 s -> 10.3 s | 180 -> 180 km/h | 10 m -> 29 m | 2.8 -> 1.2 |
  | Bavarica 530i (1,650 kg) | 6.6 -> 6.4 s | 249 -> 249 | 10 m -> 33 m | 3.3 -> 1.4 |
  | Tri-Star G63 (2,560 kg) | 4.9 -> 4.7 s | 240 -> 240 | 11 m -> 39 m | 3.0 -> 1.2 |
  | Veyrun 16.4 (1,890 kg) | 2.9 -> 2.7 s | 407 -> 407 | 9 m -> 28 m | 4.1 -> 1.6 (1.8 at 250 km/h, downforce) |
  | 530i in the rain | | | 33 -> 37 m | 1.4 -> 1.05 |

  - What changed: cars stopped in about 10 m and cornered at about 3 g (toy-like). Now: real braking by class and weight (`ClassFeel.brake`, `WeightBrake`), real tyre grip in g (`GripBase`, `GripHandling`, x `GameGrip` 1.35 so it stays fun), aero downforce growing with speed squared on every class (`Downforce`), heavier cars turn in slower and every car resists sudden direction changes at speed (`WeightInertia`, `HighSpeedYaw`), weight transfer (braking loads the front: more turn-in, `BrakeTurnIn`; braking hard mid-corner lightens the rear, `TrailBrakeLoose`; pulling away digs the rear in, `SquatGrip`), rain cuts grip 25% and braking 15% (`WetGripLoss`, `WetBrakeLoss`, from Weather), light cars bounce more on kerbs / landings / crashes (`WeightBump`). The body now leans and dives by the real g (more visible roll and nose dive); the camera keeps its old feel (`CameraG`). 0-100 and top speeds stay on each car's spec. Real kerb weights for the known cars in `CarStats` (KERB).
  - Tyre smoke: per wheel, the driven wheels most, none in the rain, dust and less of it on grass / sand, at most 100 a second per emitter, other players see it (the `Smoke` car attribute through CarSignals). Tyre marks: as wide as the tyre, darker the harder the slide, laid on the road's slope, only on hard surfaces, fade over 45 s, newest 200 kept (`SkidLife`, `SkidMax`). Tyre squeal as before.
  - Settings > DRIVING: **Driving assists Off / Low / Normal** (catches a slide past the tyres' peak, calmer phone steering at speed, `StabilityAssist`, `TouchSteerAssist`; the drift assist scales with it), **Tyre smoke Off / Low / High**, **Tyre marks On / Off**; saved and checked on the server.
  - **Lighter, calmer at top speed (owner, same day: "the car feels too heavy, make it a bit lighter", "the shaking at top speed ... feels unrealistic"):** more grip (`GameGrip` 1.35 -> 1.55), brakes about 20% stronger (Sedan 35 -> 42, SUV 31.5 -> 38, Coupe 37 -> 44, Supercar 40 -> 48, Hypercar 42 -> 50: 100-0 about 22-33 m), quicker turn-in (`WeightInertia` 0.5 -> 0.35, `HighSpeedYaw` 220 -> 320, `WeightBrake` 0.25 -> 0.2). Speed shake: a slow, small rumble (about 9 a second) instead of a fast jitter (20-40 a second): chase shake a third, Immersive road buzz 0.09 -> 0.035, the high-speed vibration 0.03 -> 0.012, sway roll 2.5 -> 1 degree and float half; crashes and kerbs still jolt. Not re-measured with the table's test; driven briefly in Studio, no errors.
  - To make it heavier / lighter: `Config.DrivingFeel.GameGrip` (lower = slides sooner), `ClassFeel.*.brake` (lower = longer stops), `HighSpeedYaw` (lower = slower turn at speed), `WeightInertia`.
  - Not measured: lane change at 250 km/h, kerb hits, jumps, crashes into traffic (traffic cars are animated, not physical, so a heavy car can't shove them), phone-size driving. Studio test hooks added: player attributes `TestCar`, `TestEngine`, `TestGearbox`, `TestTeleport`.
- **Speed effect: realistic wind blur (2026-11-03, `Prompts/Camera and car movement like the video.md`):** the hard white speed lines are gone. From 120 km/h (`WindFromMph`) to the car's top speed: a depth-of-field blur on the street ahead and the roadside further off (the car and the road close by stay sharp; Roblox has no screen-edge radial blur), a few feathered, see-through streaks drifting at the very edge of the screen (`WindStreaks`), a light edge vignette (`Vignette` 0.35); the gentle motion blur stays. Off in the cockpit, with Motion blur off or Reduced Motion; 60% on phones; the depth of field only on High / Ultra graphics. Picture (Veyrun on a test road): [[Wind blur - 100, 200, 300 kmh.jpg]]. No before pictures were taken.
- **Curve walls (2026-11-03, owner: "add a boulder at like turns on the mountain turns ... that isn't full around the curve so fix it and add boulders for wide curves"):** new `tools/riviera/CurveWalls.luau`: a low cream stone wall (the hairpins' Limestone) runs all the way round the outside of every curve on the hill roads (Mountain Road, hairpins, terraces, switchbacks) and the wide sweeping curves of the long routes, just past the pavement: 485 pieces. The hairpins' leg walls between side-by-side legs are rebuilt along their whole length (63 pieces, were 18 with gaps; the rebuilt pieces match the old ones exactly where both exist). Walls stop at junctions and tunnels. Pictures: [[Curve walls - mountain curve.jpg]], [[Curve walls - hairpin leg walls.jpg]]. Owner: save the place.
- **Traffic only on the yellow roads (2026-11-03, owner, minimap picture: "only make it so that there is traffic on the yellow roads ... the current amount of traffic on the roads is good ... just remove the cars on the roads which isn't yellow"):** traffic spawns on the three long routes (Coast Road, Town Road, Mountain Road; `seg.route` in RivieraTraffic), a car turns off a route only rarely (`LeaveRouteK` 0.03) and back onto one gladly, and one that wanders off is moved back once it's 260 studs away (`StreetRecycle`); other streets get only a rare car (`StreetShare` 0.05). Cars 90 -> 38 so the routes keep about the same number as before (measured near the harbour: 36 on the routes before; with 46 cars, 44-46 on the routes and 0-2 elsewhere after 70 s). The old hill-road share is gone (Mountain Road is a route).
- **Hole at the hotel ramp, flickering hotel ceiling (2026-11-03, owner pictures):** the venue ramps (`EntranceRamp`) hide small props standing on the drive, and they took the road's own small triangle pieces for props: 21 road pieces went invisible and walk-through at the hotel, Riviera Bay and two Coast Road venues (the hole). The road is never touched now. The hotel lobby ceiling's underside sat exactly on the podium roof's underside, so cream and charcoal flickered: the ceiling hangs 0.15 lower (`Hotel.lua`). Checked in Play: 0 hidden road pieces, 0.15 studs between the two faces.
- **Speed bumps (2026-11-03, owner: "add slow down ramps on intersection and heavy turns", then "don't add slow down ramps on yellow roads"):** new `tools/riviera/SpeedBumps.luau`: a low rounded hump (0.35 studs, 3.2 deep) in yellow and black stripes across the whole road, 26 studs before every junction on the side streets and before / after every sharp turn (over 55 degrees in ~100 studs); none on the three yellow routes, in tunnels, or within 45 studs of another: 159 bumps (`Workspace.Riviera.Dressing.SpeedBumps`, tag `SpeedBump`). Each stripe sits on the road's own height. Traffic slows to 14 mph for them (`Config.Traffic` BumpMph, BumpLook). A 530i over one at 81 km/h: a firm bump, no jump. Picture: [[Speed bump - before a junction.jpg]]. Owner: save the place.
- **No bumps at hairpins; traffic turns cleanly (2026-11-03, owner: "don't add speed ramps on the hairpin", "some cars are turning left and turning right, they're not programmed properly"):** speed bumps left out on the Hairpin and Switchbacks roads and at any hairpin bend (over 120 degrees) on other roads: 159 -> 105. Traffic turns: where a lane's end and the next lane's start sat too close for the angle, the turn curve swung one way and then back (89 of 414 junction turns did). Each turn may now leave a piece or two earlier and join a piece or two later, picking the nearest pair whose way in and way out meet ahead of both, so it's one clean arc: 2 left (the other 15 are U-turns at dead ends). Checked in Play: 38 cars running, no errors. (`tools/chauffeur_test.luau` was already failing before this change, in ChauffeurRoute line 76.)
- **Walls out of the lanes, fewer cars (2026-11-03, owner: "the side wall is on the road making the road a 2+1.5 road ... check others", "reduce a little bit of traffic like 10 cars"):** every leg wall and curve wall was checked against the traffic lanes: any piece closer than 5.5 studs to a lane's centre line (a lane is 16 wide) stood in that lane and was removed: 36 of 63 leg walls (mostly the pieces the rebuild had added where the hairpin legs meet a junction) and 6 of 485 curve walls. `CurveWalls.clearLanes` does the same after any rebuild. Traffic 38 -> 28 cars. Owner: save the place.
- **Road islands, lane lines on narrowed roads (2026-11-03, owner, picture of Upper Street meeting Harbour Street: "fix the road here and do the boulder properly, the road became 3.5 lane"):** where two roads run side by side, each one's facing side is narrowed (to ~22-26 of 32), and the lane line stayed 16 out, so that side read as a lane and a half (2 + 1.5 = "3.5 lanes"); between the two edges was a pit with the ground showing and broken bits of leg wall. Now (1) `tools/riviera/RoadLines.luau` puts the lane line in the middle of a narrowed side (two narrower lanes) and the edge line follows the edge; redone on the 16 roads that have narrowed sides; (2) `tools/riviera/RoadIslands.luau` fills each gap with an island (pavers in town, concrete elsewhere; it slopes when the roads differ in height), kerbs on both edges, and one continuous stone wall down the middle (never within 5.5 of a lane); it carries on beside the pavement while the gap lasts, clears terrain poking through, and a gap under 6 studs (a crack) gets flush asphalt instead. 15 islands (`Workspace.Riviera.Dressing.RoadIslands`), 20 wall pieces, the last 23 leg walls over them removed. Picture: `Road island - Upper and Harbour Street.jpg`. Owner: save the place.
- **Bay Link corner made straight (2026-11-03, owner drew a straight red line across the curved corner where Bay Link meets Riviera Bay: "make the road like that instead of curving it"; "remove that part which is sticking out"; "fix a little of this too"):** the road edge is now the straight line from the Riviera Bay kerb tip to the Bay Link kerb tip (`tools/riviera/StraightKerb.luau`: pavers over the old curved corner, a kerb on the line, old kerbs and lines inside removed). Behind it, the drop up to Coast Road's higher pavement got a cream stone retaining wall (it had a pit with ground showing), and the stepped old pavement pieces at the Coast Road end were replaced by one sloped piece walled round. The pointed island between Bay Link and Riviera Bay (it stuck out into the road with a pit beside it) is now flush asphalt with kerbs on the pavement ends. Bay Link's traffic lanes at the corner were moved to fit (edited in `src/shared/RivieraLanes.lua`: a re-export with `export_lanes.py` would undo it). Map parts: `Workspace.Riviera.Dressing.StraightKerbs`. Picture: `Straight kerb - Bay Link corner.jpg`. Owner: save the place.
