## How cars are picked (in order)
1. A model in `ReplicatedStorage/CarModels/<car id>` (any Creator Store model works; add a `Reverse` attribute if it faces backwards).
2. Built-in 3D meshes. Needs Game Settings → Security → "Allow Mesh / Image APIs".
3. The old block-built car.

## Good to know
- Saving progress in Studio needs "Enable Studio Access to API Services" (or a published game).
- Claude can't download Roblox models or test in real Roblox from a cloud session. It can search the Creator Store.
- There is no real rain sound yet (it uses filtered wind). A sound id can go in `Config.Sounds.Rain`.

- Sound quality upgrade (paused: Claude asks before resuming).
- Find a real rain sound.
- Balance the XP and reward numbers.

## Car fixes (2026-10-10)
- Cars are now real-world size (real wheelbase, 1 m = 3 studs).
- Imported cars use their own interior and steering wheel; the script's dash, wheel and driver are hidden.
- First-person camera sits at the model's driver seat. To nudge one car's camera: select the model in `CarModels`, add a Number attribute `EyeHeight` (e.g. `0.3` up, `-0.3` down).
- A model's steering wheel only turns if it's in a group named `SW` or has "steering wheel" in its name.
- Imported cars are saved in the game code (`Games/CityLegends/assets/CarModels.rbxm`).

First one: Ferrari 458 (`ferrara_458`), built from the owner's reference (yellow rev counter, temperature + fuel screen left, speed + clock screen right). The owner will add the 458 model and describe the other dashboards later.

  - **Cluster in the video (Gemini watched it, 2026-10-16):** round digital rev counter 1-9 (x1000 rpm) with a red needle, redline past 8,500; big red gear number with italic digital km/h speed under it, lower right inside the dial; centre of the dial shows warnings, drive mode "Corsa" and the odometer; left screen: clock, date, outside temperature; right screen: temperature bars and fuel range; angular matte-black hood. Clear from 0:00 to 0:56.

## Driving feel (2026-10-11, to match Highway Legends)
- Body leans outward in turns, nose dips on braking, squats on acceleration.
- Steering a little slower than before.
- Less grip, closer to real life; slightly slippery on hard launches.
- Outside camera shakes a little; first-person view stays steady (only crashes jolt it).
- **Camera like the reference video (2026-10-29, `Prompts/Camera and car movement like the video.md`):**
  - Cameras (V cycles): Immersive -> **Low** (new: wheel-top height, off to the side, strong blur) -> Chase -> Cockpit.
  - Outside cameras: much calmer FOV (+11 at top speed, was +38); speed shows through lag (the car pulls away flooring it, comes closer braking), falling back at speed, more motion blur. In corners the car slides out in the frame and the camera rolls up to about 4.5 degrees, back to centre in half a second.
  - Cockpit: the FOV never changes. The driver's head is on a spring: slides out and tilts the horizon (up to 6 degrees) in corners, dips forward and down braking, pushed back pulling away, looks into turns. Breathing when slow or parked.
  - High-speed sway and shake from 100 mph (every camera), growing to 190.
  - Steering wheel turns up to 300 degrees each way at parking speed, less at speed; small corrections on the straight; hands re-grip (cross over) past 100 degrees.
  - **Settings > Camera FX:** Shake (Off / Low / Normal), Head move (Off / Low / Normal), Motion blur (On / Off), FOV 60 to 90 (outside cameras). Saved with the profile. Reduced Motion players start with shake and head movement off.
  - All the numbers: `Config.Camera` (Immersive, Low, Drama, Head, Shake) and `Config.DrivingFeel` (BodyRoll, BodyPitch, WheelLockSlow, LockFadeMph, HandReach, WheelWobble).
  - Body lean still tilts the whole car (physics box too), not only the visual model.
- **McLaren steering wheel (2026-10-29, owner's photo):** flat-bottom carbon-look wheel, orange top stripe, suede grips, paddles, plain hub (no logo). Made with Studio's mesh generator: `ReplicatedStorage.SteeringWheels.McLaren` (old one kept as `McLaren_Old`). In the place only.
- **NPC gaps (2026-10-29, owner: "NPC cars on each other's tail"):** moving NPCs keep at least a car length plus half a second (`Config.Traffic` FollowGap / Headway); stopped queues still close up to 4 studs.
- **Dashboards built (2026-10-11):** LP700 Aventador (Sport screen), Ferrari 458, 330i (BMW digital Live Cockpit), M3 E92 (classic BMW like M5/M6), CLA 45 (AMG twin dials + PRNDM screen), Supra MK4 (TRD triple), Toyota (Camry, Crown, Land Cruiser, Alphard: power meter + km/h speedo). Other cars use their own needles or the generic dashboard.
- **Roster changes (2026-10-11):** E-Class W212 removed; W213 renamed to plain E200 / E300 / E400 (`bentz_e200/300/400`). Rolls Phantom replaced by Cullinan (`rolls_cullinan`, SUV). LP570 shares the LP560 model and interior (`CarBuilder.MODEL_ALIAS`). EVs changed to what the store has: BYT Dolphin EV (`byt_dolphin`), Kio EV6 (`kio_ev6`), BYT Seal EV (`byt_seal`). 52 cars now. Added 9 (2026-10-11, RX-7 in Starter): S63 W222, Maybach S680, Boxster 718, Murcielago LP640, RX-7, AMG GT63 S, Corvette C7, Corvette C6, Camaro SS: 61 cars.

  - Wheelbases match the files: GLE coupe 2.915 m, X6 (G06) 2.975 m.

    - new designs: Maybach 57 S (two chrome-ringed dials, screen between), SU7 and U8 (wide EV screen: big digital speed, P R N D, power bar, battery and range), RX-8 (big rev counter in the middle with digital speed, fuel and temperature either side), LFA (one big rev counter, 0-10, speed and gear inside), LX700 (the same Lexus look, 0-8), 718 Cayman (rev counter in the middle, speedometer left, round screen right), GT-R (rev counter in the middle, speedometer right, fuel and temperature left);
    - existing designs: GLE 53 = AMG Biturbo; S65 and Brabus G700 = W213 wide screen; M8, X7, 530i, 540i, X6 = BMW Live; M3 = BMW Classic.
    - LP750 SV: its own cluster from the owner's photo (2026-10-13): yellow middle with a wide rev arc (2-10, red from 6), big gear letter, R 1234567 A strip, blue speed and rpm digits under it, STRADA and odometer, small yellow screens either side with bar gauges.
  - The new X6 uses its own modern cabin (owner, 2026-10-13); the M5 interior swap is gone.
  - The owner allowed deleting the original GLB files in `imports` once the cars are in the game.
- **Sketchfab cars in the game (2026-10-13):** the owner imported the six FBX files and saved `CityLegendsCars.rbxl`. All 23 cars are now in `assets/CarModels.rbxm` (56 models).
  - Cars packed several to a file came in with Blender's `.001` name endings, so the game said "couldn't find 4 wheels". The car reader now takes those endings off.
  - Studio dropped 19 tinted colours (LFA paint parts, some Urus and 540i panels, Cayman calipers). `take_cars.luau --colours` put them back from the converted files, and `auto_car.py` now bakes such colours.
  - A part named `SteeringWheel_t<tilt>` is the whole wheel. Other parts with "steering wheel" in their names (the column left in the dash on the S65, X6 and X7) stay put.
  - Steering tilt set by hand: X6 23° (same wheel as the X7), LX700 20°.
  - The original GLB files in `imports` were deleted (about 450 MB), as the owner allowed. The six `cars_*.fbx` files are kept.
- **Interiors and colours (2026-10-13):**
  - Studio's importer left every converted car's MeshParts on RenderFidelity "Automatic", which swaps in rough low-detail meshes. All car parts in CarModels are now "Precise" (it can be set in the file, not by game scripts).
  - The racing-game rips (Urus, LFA, 540i/M5 CS, Cayman) tint some parts by game-set colours that aren't in the file. They are set by hand to match the renders: Urus body-colour trim yellow, LFA paint white, base plastic black, seat belts dark, Cayman calipers red.
  - G63 (2015) black first-person screen: its whole-cabin `Interior` part was double-sided around the camera, so it now draws one side only. This is the likely cause of the earlier red screen too. Not tested in Roblox yet.
- **Car controls and units (2026-10-13, owner's list):**
  - F start / stop the engine (no ignition key: the owner said not to add one), Q / E shift down / up, Left Shift clutch, P handbrake (on / off; Space still works while held), T traction control, L lights, Z / X / C left blinker / hazards / right blinker.
  - The camera moved from C to V, because C is now the right blinker.
  - Keyboard players start each drive with the engine off (press F). Touch and gamepad players start running. The dash is always on.
  - Gearbox: the first Q / E switches to manual (7 gears, a rev limiter, engine braking, and a launch when the clutch is dropped at high revs). Settings has Auto / Manual.
  - Traction control off lets the rear slide under power. Mouse shift-lock is turned off so Left Shift is free.
  - Blinkers and lights are seen by every player: the driver's client sends them (`CarSignals` remote), the server puts them on the car as `Blinker` / `Lights` attributes, and other clients flash corner lights and switch the headlights.
  - Settings → Speed: MPH / KM/H. It changes the HUD and every cluster (dial numbers, needles, digital readouts). Units and gearbox are saved with the profile.
  - Warning lights under the HUD speedometer: blinker arrows, ENGINE OFF, (P), TC OFF, LIGHTS. The gear tag shows AT / MT.
- **Driving feel (2026-10-13, owner: "jerk feeling", "slide don't slip", no redline at top speed, feel like a real car):**
  - 7 gears with a torque curve. Every shift cuts the drive for 0.2 s (0.1 s for a manual shift) and it comes back with a kick: the body rocks and the driver's head is pushed back.
  - The automatic shifts near the limiter flat out, early when cruising, and kicks down when you floor it.
  - Each car's power is worked out so its 0-60 mph matches its real time. Its own air drag makes it reach its real top speed in 7th gear at 88% of the revs, so it never hits the limiter there. (Before, cars topped out at about 75-80% of their listed top speed.)
  - No more running burnouts: with traction control on the tyres just bite. Wheelspin smoke only comes for a moment on a real launch (traction control off and flooring it from a stop, or a clutch drop). Sliding in turns and drifting are unchanged.
  - The brakes bite in over a moment. The body sits on springs (dives, squats, leans and settles). In the cockpit the driver's head moves with the g-forces.
  - Sound (owner): a loud tyre screech pulling away hard (first ~12% of top speed, quieter with traction control on), on a launch, and a short chirp when a hard 1st-3rd gear shift kicks in. It's sound only, with no smoke. Wind noise for your own car starts at walking pace and roars at speed.
  - Launch control (automatic): hold the brake and floor it at a stop, the revs hold at about 60%, then let go of the brake to launch.
  - Starting the engine (F) switches on the dash (with its needle sweep) and the headlights. Stopping it switches them off. Before the first start, the dash and lights are dark. L still toggles the lights while running.
- **Gears, EVs, reverse (2026-10-13, owner: revs climbed too fast in 3rd-5th, EVs one gear and silent, reverse too slow):**
  - Real gearbox spacing. Each gear reaches 30/45/58/69/79/89/100% of top speed (at 88% of the revs). Each higher gear pulls less, by its ratio, so acceleration falls off with speed like a real car. Checked in a simulation against the real LP750 SV: 0-124 mph 8.4 s (real 8.6). Every car reaches its full top speed in 7th at 88% of the revs, held there by a top speed limit.
  - Electric cars (`Electric = true` in Cars: Dolphin, EV6, Seal, SU7, U8): one gear "D", full pull up to about 35% of top speed then fading, no shifts, no engine sound (wind and tyres only), no exhaust pops. F switches them on silently. Q / E do nothing on them.
  - Reverse: up to a third of top speed (35 mph at most), with a brisker pull.
  - Cars still without a real model: BYT Seal EV, Toyoka Land Cruzer 300, Dodger SRT, Bentlee Continental GT, Bentlee Bentayga, Rolls Royal Phantom.
- **Roster (2026-10-13):** 62 cars.
  - BYT Dolphin EV removed; owners lose it from their saves.
  - Mercer GT63 S renamed "Dodger SRT". It keeps the id `bentz_gt63s` so owners keep it, and takes Charger SRT Hellcat-like stats: 196 mph, 0-60 in 3.6 s, wheelbase 3.052 m.
  - Rolls Royal Cullinan renamed "Rolls Royal Phantom". It keeps the id `rolls_cullinan`, becomes a Sedan, 0-60 in 5.1 s. Wheelbase 3.772 m, because the owner's model is the Phantom Extended.
- **New cars (2026-10-13, owner: "New car SL65 amg and M4"):** 65 cars.
  - Mercer SL65 Black Series (`bentz_sl65bs`, High, $700,000, 199 mph, 0-60 in 3.8 s) and BNW M4 F82 (`bnw_m4_f82`, Mid, $100,000, 174 mph, 4.1 s).
  - Nisun Skyline R34 GT-R (`nisun_r34`, Mid, $180,000, 165 mph, 4.9 s): its model came in the same push, so it was added as a new car too.
  - Real models for 6 more cars from the same push: Dodger SRT (a Charger Hellcat), Bentlee Bentayga, Rolls Royal Phantom, Rolls Royal Wraith, BYT Seal and Toyoka Land Cruzer 300. Still without a real model: Bentlee Continental GT.

- **Lighting (2026-10-13, owner: "Make all of the game lighting way better"):** a rainy neon night look, like Highway Legends.
  - The base light is now a dimmer, bluer moonlight, so street lamps, neon and headlights carry the scene. The moon sits a little off overhead, so buildings cast long, crisp shadows.
  - Glossy paint and wet roads reflect the city more.
  - Bright things get a wider glow (bloom): lamps, neon and lights, but not paint.
  - The colour grade has more contrast and colour, with cool shadows. The far city fades into a deep blue haze, and faint moon rays shine through the towers.
  - Street lamps are warmer and about twice as bright, with a wider pool of light on the road. Lamps within ~170 studs of the camera cast real shadows (poles, cars, players), but not on low graphics or on phones.
  - Headlights reach further (80 studs) and are a little brighter.
  - Rain still blends its darker, greyer mood on top of all this.

- **Watching videos (2026-10-16):** the owner pointed to `bradautomates/claude-video` (a "watch" skill: frames + transcript from a video). YouTube can't be fetched from the cloud session: the network proxy blocks YouTube's video hosts (403), and YouTube asks to sign in as a bot check, which would need the owner's account cookies (never used). Video files work: push a short `.mp4` (under 100 MB) into `Games/CityLegends/imports` and Claude pulls frames out with ffmpeg. At the owner's request the `watch` plugin is installed in the cloud session (local engine, tested on a file). It is set up again for each new cloud session. YouTube links could work through its Gemini engine if the owner adds a free `GEMINI_API_KEY` as an environment variable in the cloud environment settings (never pasted into chat).
  - **Gemini connected (2026-10-16):** the cloud environment now passes a Gemini key (Claude never sees it). YouTube links go through watch's Gemini engine: Google watches the video, not the cloud session. Tested on the Aventador video: works.
  - **Installed every session (2026-10-16, owner's OK):** `.claude/hooks/install-watch.sh` runs at the start of each cloud session (like RTK). It installs `watch@claude-video` pinned to v0.3.2 (no silent updates), installs `yt-dlp`, and writes `~/.config/watch/.env` set to Gemini with model `gemini-3.8-flash` (the default `gemini-3.7-flash` was overloaded; `gemini-2.5-flash` is closed to new users). The key in that file is a placeholder: the network proxy adds the real one. A first try without the owner's clear OK was blocked by the session's safety check.
  - **How Claude uses it:** `python3 ~/.claude/plugins/cache/claude-video/watch/0.3.2/skills/watch/scripts/watch.py "<link or file>" --question "<question>"`. Use this if the `/watch` skill isn't listed in the session. If Gemini says "high demand" (503), retry later or with `WATCH_GEMINI_MODEL=gemini-3.7-flash`.
  - **Other sites (checked 2026-10-17):** the network blocks Vimeo, TikTok, Google Drive, Dropbox, Streamable, Twitch, Dailymotion, Imgur and Reddit video. YouTube links (through Gemini) and video files pushed to the repo work.

Drive-mode words (STRADA, SPORT) stay. Real logos printed in the car models' own textures (steering-wheel badges and so on) are not touched yet.

NPC traffic drives the highway too, faster than in town; only right turns at the junction.

Open: the owner says most cars' wheels need fixing and most interiors are grey (screenshots asked for); the Malibu's dash came out crumpled from thinning; the C63 file has cabin and body merged by material, so only a few cabin pieces carry over to the CLA 45 and GLE until its cabin is split out.

  - **No real badges on them either:** badge parts hidden, printed logos and name plates covered, BNW / Mercer / Lexis emblems where those badges were, wheel caps and steering-wheel hubs covered (checked in Blender renders). The S-Class turned out to be an S63, so it is named **Mercer S63 W221**.
  - **Tail lights:** each new model's own red lamp parts glow (or lamp spots picked on its rear for the Murcielago, 190E, GLS 600 and LC500, whose lamps are painted or part of a whole-car piece).
  - Converter fixes: wheels must sit out at the car's sides (the Murcielago's seats were taken for wheels), and the car is measured before its quick thinning (the Malibu's shrank into a 10 m wide car). The GLS 600's front bumper came out with a torn piece hanging down; it was cut off.
- **Big update plan (2026-10-22, owner):** fix the car models (wheels, grey interiors, C63 cockpit), an immersive camera that feels faster, getting out of the car and walking, AI police with speed cameras (limit 120 km/h) and chases, a bank to rob, towers with penthouses (GTA 5 style apartments, plus a DJ booth and party mode; no alcohol anywhere), a car stock market that looks like the owner's Flip Rush market (screenshot: live news ticker, Requests / Stalls / Flip-off, offer banner with PASS / SELL, Listings with BUY and hold-to-lowball, Stash with fees, hype and sparklines), and more GTA-style things to do (no clubs). Flip Rush is a website (`fliprush.site`); the cloud session's network blocks it, so the screenshot is the reference.
  - **Wheel problem found:** each converted wheel (tyre, rim, disc...) has several materials, but Studio makes it one part with one texture, so wheels came out painted with a single texture; the steering wheel too. The wheel meshes also took in calipers, arch liners, suspension and body bits, which spun with the wheel.
  - **Immersive camera (V cycles Immersive / Chase / Interior; Immersive is the default):** low and close behind the car, the view widens with speed (74 to 112 FOV), thrown back on launch and forward under braking, swings into turns, road buzz at speed, blur and speed lines from the screen edges. Tuning in `Config.Camera.Immersive`.
  - **Walking:** Y (or the GET OUT / GET IN button) gets out when the car is nearly stopped and back in beside it; the car stays parked. Your own Roblox avatar; dying on foot puts you back beside your car. A car from the garage puts you straight in it.
  - Not tested in Roblox yet.
  - **Police and speed cameras:** speed cameras (pole, yellow box, a 120 sign) stand along the city avenues and both shoulders of the Legends Loop. Passing one above 123 km/h: white flash, shutter, a fine ($500 + $25 per km/h over) and 1 star. Patrol cars (black Crowne Athlete with a light bar and POLICE on the doors) drive the streets and call you in if you speed past them. Wanted: 1 + stars units hunt you, following the roads from far away and driving straight at you when close and in sight, sirens and light bars on, red/blue glow at the screen edges. Stopped next to a police car for 3 s: BUSTED (6% of your cash, $500-$150k). Out of sight and 300 studs away for 9-18 s: ESCAPED (bounty $1.5k-$20k and XP). Ramming a police car adds a star; a 75 s chase adds one (up to 3). Tuning in `Config.Police`.
  - **Exhaust sounds:** the owner will add exhaust sounds per car with one sound per RPM band; Claude blends them by RPM once the owner sends the asset ids.
  - **Car stock market (styled after the Flip Rush market screenshot):** open with K or the MARKET side button. Cars trade as stock in four conditions (Barn find, Driven, Clean, Showroom) with live server prices that move every 15 s and jump on brand or class news ("Lambrini ▲ ..."). Listings: tap BUY, or hold to lowball. Stash: up to 32 units with profit and a price chart; sell to the market for value minus a fee (14%, lower with hype), or to a buyer's timed offer (no fee). Three daily requests pay 1.4-1.8x for a car in a condition or better. Hype (0-10) rises with good flips: better offers, lower fees, more lowballs land. Flip-off: 3 minutes to out-earn Slick Vinny for a cash prize. A Showroom unit can be delivered to your garage as the real car. Saved in the profile. Tuning in `Config.Market`; logic test `tools/market_test.luau`.
  - **Bank robbery:** the Legends Reserve Bank stands on city block (1, 2): steps, six columns, a marble hall with chandeliers and teller counters, and a round steel vault door at the back. On foot, hold E at the vault door for 8 s: the alarm rings, red lights flash, the door swings open for 90 s and you get 4 stars. Cash carts in the vault: hold E (2.5 s) to stuff your bag ($6k-$11k a grab, more with level, up to $60k). The loot rides with you on foot or in your car; an arrow points to the drop-off (a green beam at the corner of block (5, 0)). Lose the police, then reach the drop-off to cash in (plus 400 XP). Busted: the loot is gone. The vault then stays locked for 5 minutes. Tuning in `Config.Bank`.
  - **Car models fixed:** wheels and steering wheels came into Studio as one part with one texture (blank grey). Now one part per material. Calipers steer without spinning; arch liners and body bits stay on the body. Tiled and second-UV textures, see-through texture spots and metal maps fixed (the cause of many grey interiors). The C63 cabin is split out so the CLA 45 and GLE get its whole cockpit. Malibu dash reconverted with more detail. All 52 converted cars repacked as `imports/new_cars/cars_21` to `cars_34`; the owner must re-import all 14, then Claude copies them into `CarModels`.
  - **Luxury towers:** Aurum Residences (block 2,3; $750K, $12K a day rent), Skyline One (4,1; $3M, $45K) and The Crown (1,4; $12M, $160K). Walk-in lobby with a revolving door, concierge, sales desk (hold to buy), elevators (lobby / penthouse / roof). Penthouse at the top with real city views: TV lounge, kitchen, bedroom ("Set as home"), wardrobe, wall safe (daily rent), car showcase turntable, DJ booth with party mode, confetti popper. Roof: sky pool, telescope, helipad with a helicopter city tour. Anyone can visit; owners get the safe, a home spawn and their name on the plaque. No alcohol. Tuning in `Config.Towers`.
  - **Things to do:** 4 time-trial races (Downtown Sprint, City Ring, Zigzag, Crosstown): stop in an orange ring to start, 3-2-1-GO, checkpoints at every junction, gold / silver / bronze by average speed ($9k / $5k / $2.5k), best times saved. Delivery jobs: stop in a blue ring (Dockside or Uptown Dispatch), drive to a drop point across town before the clock runs out (pay by distance plus time left). 25 Legends chips: spinning gold chips on the sidewalks, $2,500 each and $100,000 for all of them. Tuning in `Config.Activities`.
  - Flip Rush market note: the owner sent a screenshot of the Flip Rush market (fliprush.site) as the look for the car stock market.
- **Exhaust sounds per car (2026-10-22, owner; redline changed to 15989284563 the same day):** first test on the Aventador SV LP750-4 with the owner's uploads: start-up, idle, mid rpm, high rpm, launch control, crackle, redline. Idle / mid / high are loops crossfaded by rpm (pitch follows the revs), the start-up plays on F and the idle fades in after it, launch control plays while brake + throttle are held at a stop, crackles pop on lift-off at high revs and on upshifts, and the redline loop plays on the rev limiter. Ids live in `Config.Sounds` as `Exhaust_<car id>_<Start | Idle | Mid | High | Launch | Crackle | Redline>`; any car with them uses its own exhaust. Other players hear it from your car too.
- **Car series and how you get cars (2026-10-22, owner; replaces the earlier trim idea):**
  - The garage groups cars into series (e.g. BNW 5 SERIES: 530i, 540i, M5 E60, M5 F10, M5 F90; BNW 3 & 4 SERIES: 330i, M3 E92, M3 F80, M4 F82; MERCER E SERIES: E300, E400, E63 S). The first car of a series is open from the start; each next one needs 5 more levels (level 5, 10, 15, 20). Locked cars show a lock and "LVL n".
  - From the Ferrara F430 up ($600k and over) cars are market-only: find a Showroom unit on the car market (K) and take delivery; a car later in a series needs the one before it owned (e.g. the Aventador SV needs the LP700-4). About 1 in 5 market listings is a market-only car you could unlock next, often in Showroom condition; prices run under and over value.
  - New cars: BNW M5 F10 ($145k) and M5 F90 ($165k) on the G30 5 Series model for now, Mercer E63 S ($175k) on the E-Class model (which is an E63 S). They need their own models later if the owner wants. Named "Mercer E63 S" (no AMG, the brand mark rule).
  - Series and rules live in `Cars.Series`, `Cars.MarketFromPrice`, `Cars.requiredLevel`, `Cars.previous`; the dealer and the market check them on the server.

- **High cars harder to get, garage by brand (2026-10-22, owner: "make the high cars harder to get in the market and also fit each car into the brands"):**
  - Market-only cars ($600k and up) show up less: about 1 in 12 listings is a car you could unlock next (was 1 in 5), and only about 15% of those are Showroom (the only condition that can be delivered). Other listings of market-only cars are rarely Showroom, and their asking prices run from 95% to 160% of value (mostly over).
  - The garage lists brands (BNW, MERCER, TOYOKA, LAMBRINI ...), each with its series under it. The brand is the make in the car's name (`Series.Brand` in `Cars`).
- **M5 E60 and M6 needles (2026-10-22, owner screenshot):** the red needles reached past the dials; they're now two-thirds as long (`CarSkin.NEEDLE_LENGTH`).

- **Fix list (2026-10-22, owner):**
  - Alfard: it gets the Land Cruzer 200's cluster design, placed over its own dials, and the Land Cruzer 300's steering wheel (its own has the maker's logo printed on it).
  - Land Cruzer 200: its cluster now sits over the model's own dial panel ("bring it down and match it exactly"), not above the dash.
  - The Chevo Malibo is gone from the game (roster, garage, model). Saves that owned it just lose it. Pack `cars_21` still holds it: skip it when copying cars in.
  - X6: it takes the 7 Series' (750Li) steering wheel. The fixed X6 and 750Li models are in packs `cars_30` and `cars_31`, still to be imported.
  - No logos anywhere (2026-10-22, owner: "remove all of the logo in the game"): the fictional BNW / Mercer / Lexis emblems are off too; badge spots get plain covers. On Roblox-built cars, a small logo piece in the middle of the steering wheel is taken off. Replaces the 2026-10-17 emblem decision.
  - 330i steering wheel: its own wheel came in as one part with one texture, so parts of it don't show. The fixed 330i is in pack `cars_28`, still to be imported.
  - Police: getting caught takes 10% of the cash you have, with no cap (was 6%, $500 to $150k).
  - Wider roads: lanes 16 studs (were 12). Every road, the highway and the loop are a third wider; blocks are a little smaller.

- **The west, Downtown and the islands (2026-10-22, owner: Miami / New York downtown, coastal highway over a river on a suspension bridge, private islands, casino and gem showcase, chauffeur, hotel with valets and per-minute penthouses, houses with car garages, pedestrians, time of day):**
  - **Getting there:** leave the city west (Legends Blvd, the middle road). The coastal highway climbs onto a viaduct over the harbour, the suburbs and the Legends Loop, drops into the countryside, runs through a canyon in the mountains and crosses the river on the **Legends Bridge** (lit towers, necklace lights on the cables) into **Downtown**.
  - **Downtown:** its own 6 x 6 street grid with traffic lights and traffic, glass skyscrapers, palms on every sidewalk, a skyline ring, a riverside park with a big DOWNTOWN sign. Along the beach, **Ocean Drive**: pastel art deco hotels with neon names, cafes, the promenade, the beach (lifeguard towers, parasols) and the sea.
  - **Islands:** the causeway from Ocean Drive goes to **Legends Key** (ring road, fountain, helipad), with bridges on to **Palm Isle** and **Coral Cay**.
  - **Legends Grand Hotel** (Ocean Drive, by the boulevard): valets (stop in the yellow VALET box, press Y; get the car back at the VALET stand with E), lobby, roof. Suites pay every full minute you're in the game: Skyline $2M ($2,000/min), Ocean $6M ($6,500/min), Royal $12M ($14,000/min), Penthouse $25M ($30,000/min). Since 2026-11-03 it is one suite you upgrade (see the hotel suites section below).
  - **High-Rollers Casino** (across the boulevard): scenery-only gaming floor (Roblox bans playable gambling, even with fake money), VIP cards Gold $250k / Platinum $2.5M / Black $25M (title over your head, VIP lounge, gem discount), and a vault heist (security panel, vault door, trolleys, 5 stars, up to $400k, cashed in at the bank's drop-off). `Config.Luxe.GamingFloorDecor = false` swaps the slot machines for lounge seating if the age rating needs it.
  - **Legends Gem Showcase** (on the boulevard): 19 wearable flex pieces in 6 slots (necklaces, watches, rings, earrings, shades, crowns), $15k up to the $40M Midnight Star; everyone sees what you wear. Smash-and-grab: cases (3 stars) and the Legends Heart dome ($75k, 4 stars).
  - **Chauffeur:** H (or the CHAUFFEUR button) on foot, or a gold stand. A contract per car is bought once (the owner's fleet prices: DBX $450k up to the Phantom $2.5M); rides are free after that. Pick a place; the car drives you there on the roads; SKIP RIDE / STOP HERE.
  - **Island houses:** 12 Island Villas ($4M, 4-car glass garage) on Palm Isle / Coral Cay, 6 Legends Key Mansions ($15M, 6-car garage). Hold E at the FOR SALE sign; pick which owned cars stand in the bays (E at the garage screen); E on a bay car drives it out.
  - **Pedestrians** walk the city and Downtown sidewalks, cross on red, stroll the promenade (fewer on phones).
  - **Time of day:** one full day every 24 minutes, the same for everyone: night, pink dawn, morning, day, gold sunset, violet evening (`Config.DayCycle`).
  - **StreamingEnabled is ON since 2026-10-29** (owner: "I can't even load it up"; the map is about 168,000 parts and every player loaded all of them). A player now gets about 25,000 to 33,000 parts near them. The far skyline (`City.DistantSkyline.AlwaysLoaded`) is always loaded; the skyline ring and 175 city / Downtown buildings show low-detail stand-ins when far (`LevelOfDetail = StreamingMesh`). **2026-10-30:** those stand-ins showed as black shapes (owner: "fix that black thing throughout the map and on the roads"), so they were swapped for our own always-loaded plain copies with lit floors (`City.Standins`), and wet roads no longer turn black at night. Shadow trim baked into the place (flat, tiny and far parts cast none). Code: a stream focus for the menu, pre-loading before spawns and teleports, cars arrive whole (Atomic), knockables and the VIP gate pick up parts that stream in later.
  - Built and checked in a Roblox emulator (Lune): the whole map builds without errors, every lane has road under it and nothing solid in it, and every chauffeur route between places is found. Not yet run in real Roblox.

- **Fixes and additions (2026-10-23, owner):**
  - Police: at most 3 cars chase you (2 patrols when you're not wanted). Every chase has a timer under the stars ("POLICE GIVE UP IN 0:40": 40 s at 1 star up to 80 s at 5); when it runs out, or you lose them, you get a small bounty ($400 to $4,000). Getting caught still takes 10% of your cash.
  - Despawn: the DESPAWN side button or the Delete key puts your car away (you get out first); G brings one back.
  - LP750: our drawn cluster is gone; the model's own cluster shows as it is (`CarSkin.KEEP_OWN_CLUSTER`). Replaces the earlier "AventadorSV" design drawn from the owner's photo.
  - Penthouse and hotel suite turntables: residents press E "Change car" and pick which of their cars is on show (saved per penthouse, `Showcase` in the save). The island houses already had this (Garage setup).
  - Less neon ("too cyberpunk"): signs mostly warm white and amber, all neon a little less saturated and bright, fewer lit roof outlines, white or warm crowns, a calmer glow, natural colour grading (no boosted saturation), a less blue night.
  - Water: deeper sea colour, a little see-through, real swell; its colour follows the time of day.
  - Mini map (bottom left, turns with the camera, zooms with speed): roads, landmarks, police, race and delivery targets. N hides it; on a phone tap it to make it bigger.
  - Driving feel: the car's turn builds up instead of snapping, grip falls off in a slide, power oversteer with traction control off, downforce on supercars, slower steering at speed, bumps on kerbs. At speed the view widens, the screen edges darken, wind and road roar rise; launches, flat-out shifts and close passes kick the screen. The cabin view gets only a light version.
  - Lighter map: about 151,000 parts (was 164,000; simpler traffic light visors).
  - Car colours (Maybach S680 interior, grey interiors, tyres, LFA steering wheel, 330i / X6 wheels): the fixed models are in the import packs `imports/new_cars/cars_21` to `cars_34`, still to be imported by the owner. The game can't change those textures; only a new Import 3D can.
  - LFA cluster (owner's photo, 2026-10-23): the real LFA screen: a white rev ring with black numbers 0-10 (squeezed below 4000, red zone 8,750-9,900), a red needle that follows the revs, the speed, unit, gear and SPORT in the middle, coolant / fuel arcs and 180°F on the left, oil temperature / pressure arcs and the clock on the right (`DashGauges` `lfa`).
  - **Legends Motors dealership (2026-10-23, owner: "add a dealership, a real location", SHOP renamed CARS showing only your cars):** a glass showroom on city block {4,2}, east of the centre, facing the city, with 8 brand zones and spinning cars, a sales desk and a DRIVE OUT bay. Press E at the desk or a car's stand to browse and buy (level locks and MARKET ONLY as before); new cars can only be bought there (within 260 studs). The CARS button (or G) lists only your own cars. Legends Motors is a chauffeur and minimap place.
- **Environment quality pass (2026-10-23, owner: "improve the quality of the environment"):** tower windows are dark glass by day and only glow at night; street, park, shore and beach lamps switch off in the morning and back on at dusk; clouds white by day; natural grass, trees, hills, grey rock mountains, concrete sidewalks and kerbs; a sandstone canyon; fuller palms with fewer parts; the far skyline is blue-grey glass. About 3,900 fewer parts.
- **Free-roam challenges (2026-10-23):** drift scoring (slide at 30+ mph; the multiplier climbs to x5 while you link the drift; straighten up to bank it as cash and XP; a crash loses it). Six speed traps (yellow-and-chrome gantries: Harbour Run, Legends Loop, Harbour Viaduct, Canyon Pass, Legends Bridge, Ocean Drive): bronze 100 / silver 140 / gold 180 mph, each new medal pays once ($750 / $2,000 / $5,000), bests saved, gantry shows the server record; they never call the police. Four jump ramps (two on the beach, the riverside park, Legends Key) pay for air time and distance. Leaderboard (top speed, best drift) on the city-centre corner, or press J. Yellow minimap blips.
- **Driving physics v2 and nitro (2026-10-23):** cars keep their momentum through turns (the tyres pull the slide back up to their grip), so slides come naturally; handbrake drifts you can hold and link with a countersteer assist; front-drive cars understeer on power, rear-drive cars step out, all-wheel drive in between (`Config.DrivingFeel` FrontDrive / AllDrive); weight and gearbox by class (SUVs lean and push wide, hypercars flat and quick-shifting); ABS pulsing; landings thump; crashes jolt; wall scrapes throw sparks. Nitro was built but the owner had it removed (2026-10-23: "remove nitro"); it is switched off with `Config.DrivingFeel.NitroEnabled = false`. Tyre smoke and fading skid marks (less on phones).
- **Legends Customs (2026-10-23):** a drive-in tuning workshop on city block {3,1}, one block north of the centre. Drive into the middle TUNING bay, press Y (or tap CUSTOMISE): paint (16 colours or your own), finish (gloss / matte / metallic), second colour, window tint (light / dark / limo), underglow (8 colours), rim colour, headlights (white / warm / ice) and your own plate (8 letters, text-filtered). Live preview with an orbiting camera; BUY to keep. Base prices $1,000-$4,000 a change, x2.5 for mid cars and x6 for high cars. Saved per car, seen by everyone, also on penthouse turntables and house garages. Imported models only take paint on parts named as paint, so some barely change until re-imported.
- **Who does the work (2026-10-23, owner):** the owner now gives the building work to Claude Code on their PC (started in the vault, `C:\Users\USER\ProjectOne`). The cloud Claude chat is an assistant: answers, writes prompt files (`City Legends/Prompts/`), and helps with steps. The fixed 530i alone is in `imports/new_cars/bnw_530i_only.fbx` (also in `cars_30`).
- **Play-test fixes (2026-10-23, Claude Code on the PC, tested in Studio):** wheels went missing at random (the body went on before the wheel joints arrived; the server now stamps a `Pieces` count and the client waits for it). Minimap moved to the top right (owner); daily gift card bottom left on PC, under the map on touch; race/job card under the map; GET OUT pill left of the rev counter; garage stats no longer hidden by DRIVE; symbols Gotham can't draw (close ✕, ♛, ➜, ▮, ▸) swapped. Drift cash needs a rolling car and jump cash needs a ramp nearby (stops cheats). Traffic cars on imported models leave out the cabin (owner: optimisation). Every car's windows get the same clear, lightly tinted glass (some were milky white). First-person eye: under the roof line by a sixth of the roof height, always over the steering wheel. Camera: slides out and banks in turns, tips up and widens pulling away, dives and tightens braking (`Config.Camera.Drama`; a touch of it in the driver's seat).
- **Found, not fixed yet (2026-10-23):** the starter Suzaki Swiff model is made of plain boxes (looks cheap: needs a new model). Six of the owner's texture packs were rejected by Roblox moderation ("not compliant": steering wheels / badges on Aston, Mercer, BNW 3/5 series, Ferrara, Lambrini Countach). Many interiors are plain white or grey (textures missing). The Ferrara California still shows "Ferrari"/"California" text on its dash.
- **How Claude works on this PC (2026-10-23):** Windows Smart App Control blocks Rojo and Lune here, so Claude edits the scripts in the vault and copies them straight into the open Studio place (a local file server; Studio's HTTP is switched on only for the copy). The owner saves in Studio (Ctrl+S) to update `CityLegends.rbxl`. luau-lsp works. Obsidian Git backs up and pushes the work every few minutes.
- **Map baked into the place (2026-10-23):** the whole scenery (roads, buildings, trees, lamps, signals, skyline, mainland, the west, bridge, islands, water) is now saved in `CityLegends.rbxl` under Workspace → City, so it shows in Studio without pressing Play and can be edited there. The venues (bank, Legends Motors, Legends Customs, towers, hotel, casino, gem shop, houses, challenges) are still built by script at start, into the baked map. Start-up build went from 2.2 s to 0.3 s; same size (~136,000 baked parts + ~16,000 at start). Every lane has road under it and nothing in the way (checked with `tools/bake_check.luau`). Rule: map edits made in Studio are the owner's; Claude edits the map only when asked, in the Studio place, and never rebuilds the place with Rojo over it.
- **Cluster photos (2026-10-23, owner):** real cluster photos are in `Games/CityLegends/imports/refs/` (README lists each one and its source): Veyron (lit Grand Sport Vitesse cluster, Commons dash shots), Aventador LP700-4 (Commons lit photo, YouTube start-up and STRADA frames), Aventador SV (YouTube start-up frames: yellow screen). The Veyron got its own cluster from them (`DashGauges` `Veyron`: five silver-ringed dials, power 0-1001 hp left, rev counter 0-8 middle with a small screen, speedometer to 280 mph right, blue bands, red needles); the LP700 cluster got a cyan odometer and amber screen rules. The SV keeps its model's own cluster. assetto-fr.com was not used: it re-hosts paid Assetto Corsa mods without the makers' permission. YouTube frames (real cars, Forza, Assetto Corsa gameplay) are fine as references.
- **Big update (2026-10-23, Claude Code on the PC, owner's list):**
  - **Traffic lights:** one head per road, over the inner lane, facing the oncoming cars (owner picked "simpler heads"); the extra per-lane heads and the low pole heads are gone (map and code).
  - **Trees:** street and park trees have a tapered trunk, two branches and a full two-tone crown; the mainland firs have four stepped tiers (map and code).
  - **Block_5_2 fountain:** a cobbled round plaza, an eight-sided stone basin with lit water, a two-tier centre piece with spray, arcing jets, flower beds and benches (`Fountain.lua`, used by every park).
  - **Start:** a first car comes out on the street beside Block_3_2 (the Needle's block); on foot with no car you stand up on its sidewalk (`Config.Spawn`). A car called from the garage while you're on foot comes out right in front of you when there's room; swapping cars while driving keeps you where you are.
  - **Grey blocks filled (owner: the empty grey city blocks):** the landmark and venue blocks (Legends Tower, Needle, Twist Tower, the bank, the three penthouse towers) get stone-tiled plazas with food trucks, an art ring, a basketball court, gardens, big screens and kiosks; entrances stay clear (`Plazas.lua`). Customs and the dealership are left as they were.
  - **UI:** the daily gift card, minimap, leaderboard, race/job card and bank loot card have an X; a MAP pill brings the minimap back; clicking the minimap opens a full map (scroll / + - to zoom, drag to move).
  - **Knock-downs:** street lights, trees and traffic light poles fall over when you hit them at speed (a local physics copy; other players see it too) and stand back up after 40 s once no car is near (`Knockables.lua`, `Config.Knock`).
  - **Chauffeur:** rides the lane at its own height like the traffic cars (no more flying or sinking), snaps back if physics pulls it off, and drives faster: 62 mph in town, 125 on the highway.
  - **Car logos:** decals showing makers' logos on the Roblox-built cars (Toyota, BMW, M / M5, BBS, Suzuki, Porsche, Lamborghini, "mazda", "Bathurst R", an RX-7 plate) are dropped (`CarSkin` LOGO_IMAGES); parts named "lettering" go too. Still showing: logos painted into converted cars' own textures (e.g. the SV side stripe, the California dash text): they need a texture edit and a new Import 3D.
  - **Tower garages (owner: basement parking you can drive down, parking at the entrance, VIP paid parking, cars to steal):** behind Aurum Residences, Skyline One and The Crown a driveway leads to a ramp down into a lit basement under the block (P -1: 108 bays, pillars, signs) (`Garages.lua`). Three gold "VIP PARKING" bays by each entrance charge $500 when you leave your car there (towed if you can't pay). Eight random Starter/Mid cars are parked in each basement: hold E (2.5 s) to steal one, you drive it out with 1 police star; it's never added to your garage, and a new random car fills the bay after 90-180 s (`Parking.lua`, `Config.Parking`). The hotel and casino don't have one yet.
  - Tested in Studio: the clusters (rendered), the spawn spots, the minimap close and full map, the knock-down physics, the steal (parked R34 became a drivable stolen car with a star). Not tested by driving: the knock-downs while driving, the VIP fee, the chauffeur at speed. The map changes are in the open Studio place: save it (Ctrl+S) to keep them.
- **Windows (2026-10-23, owner):** every car's windshield and windows are black SmoothPlastic at 0.9 transparency (imported models, runtime meshes and the part-built cars). Legends Customs' tint still darkens them from there.

They're 30 tall now (1 stud under the deck), in the Studio map and in the code. A sweep of every highway and Downtown road found nothing else hidden in the lanes.
  - **Car glitches:** the body was always held level, so on the highway's climbs and ramps its nose or tail dug into the road, physics shoved it back, the game took that as a crash and the car jolted and bounced. The car now lies along the road's slope (smoothed; kerb sides don't count). Nearby traffic cars now tell the physics their speed (they were moved by teleporting, so a touch could fling you). Knocked-down poles and trees are light, so the car pushes them aside instead of riding up on them. Not driven yet (Studio ignores test key presses).

Every car mesh uses Roblox's automatic detail levels (was "Precise", full detail at any distance): triangles ~3M to ~0.85M. Street lights switch off past 560 studs (was 900); the 60 speed camera flash lights are off except during a flash; chauffeur stand lights switch by distance. Parked cars in the tower basements are only drawn within 320 studs. Bolt-sized parts are left off traffic cars. Studio test fps doesn't show the gain well (Studio runs server, client and the editor at once).

New palette: graphite panels, a refined blue accent, gold for levels and rewards, softer money green (was neon cyan and hot pink). The level bar is now a compact glass pill with the level in a gold badge and a thin gold XP bar. The garage's car preview is lit properly (it was nearly black). "Press F to start up" moved up so it no longer covers the GET OUT button.

- **SV cluster, launch control, suspension, steering wheels (2026-10-24, owner):**
  - **Aventador SV cluster redone from the real one** (video frames in `imports/refs`): the wide yellow screen (flat top, straight sides, cut in to a narrow bottom), the rev band a flat wide sweep from 2 low on the left over the top to 10 low on the right (white bars light up to the revs, red from 8,500), numbers inside it, big italic gear, R 1234567 A, STRADA, odometer, "Fasten Seatbelt"; mph / rpm tags and white digits outside; clock box and bar gauges left, range box and car picture right. The old one's arc was too round.
  - **Launch control:** it now latches: brake + throttle at a stop holds the car dead still with steady revs until you let go of the brake. It used to drop out whenever the car crept, so the revs jumped 1,000-3,000 and the car rolled. Holding the brake with the throttle down no longer tries to reverse.
  - **Suspension (bridge "rocks", braking bounce, glitching near the bridge):** the car's physics body was a box sliding on the road; tipped by braking or a change of slope its edge dug in and got shoved out. Now a spring holds the box 0.45 studs above the road (`Config.DrivingFeel` RideClearance / RideSpring / RideDamp; a `Ride` VectorForce while driving). Small ground changes no longer kick the body. Reverse waits 0.4 s after a stop so a hard stop doesn't bounce straight into reverse. The bridge road surfaces checked clean (no overlaps, gaps or steps).
  - **Steering wheels remodelled for every car** (owner: "re model all cars steering wheel to match like the actual real life one but without the logos and badges"): 25 designs made with Studio's mesh generator from descriptions of the real wheels (no logos), kept in `ReplicatedStorage.SteeringWheels` and fitted to each car's own column, size and tilt (`CarSkin.STEER_MODEL`, `STEER_FIT`). Designs: AventadorFlat (LP700, SV, Urus), LamboRound (Gallardo, Murcielago), Countach, FerrariF430 (F430, 599, California), Ferrari458, McLaren, AudiR8, Veyron, LexusSport (LFA, LC500, Levante), Porsche (GT3, Boxster, Panamera), GTR, BMWM (every BNW), AMGFlat (AMG Mercers), MercLuxury (S-Class, Maybach, E300), Merc90s (190E, W221), Rolls, Bentley, Aston, AmericanFlat (Corvettes, Camaro), Dodge, JDM90s (Supra, Chaser, R34, RX-7, 350Z), RX8, ToyotaModern (Toyotas, LX700), EV, Compact. Brand names in the prompts got some refused by moderation, so those were described without them. They turn with the steering like the old wheels. Not checked in every single car's cabin yet (checked: SV, F430).
  - Save the place (Ctrl+S): the steering wheel designs live in the place.
- **Realistic suspension (2026-10-24, owner: "add realistic suspension"):** each wheel reads the road under it. The body sits at its real ride height on the four wheels (the earlier single spring held it 0.45 studs up, so the wheels floated a little), tilts with the plane the four wheels are on (one end or side up a kerb tips it), and each wheel moves up and down on its own over bumps and kerbs (travel limits per class). The springs work through the drive constraint's vertical speed, which the physics holds every step, so it doesn't overshoot at low frame rates; they follow the road's climb on ramps. Per class (`Config.DrivingFeel.Suspension`): SUVs soft with long travel, supercars and hypercars firm and short. The body's collision box is a separate hull starting `HullLift` (0.9 studs) above the road, so walls and cars still hit it but the road doesn't. Tested in Studio: standing (all four wheels exactly on the road, body at design height, steady), dropped 1.5 studs (lands, dips, one small rebound, settled in about 0.6 s), half on a kerb (body tilts 4.7°, every wheel on its own surface). Not driven yet.

Every car has a front passenger seat; sedans, SUVs and most coupes also have two rear seats (two-seat coupes listed in `Config.Passengers`). Y or jump gets out beside that door. Tested with a stand-in car.

  - **Chauffeur:** stuck behind a slower car it moves onto the line between lanes (or the centre line), passes, then moves back into its lane. Traffic cars don't brake for it there.
  - **Island bridges:** now 2 lanes each way like the causeway (owner chose the island bridges, not Legends Bridge). Rebuilt in the baked map in Studio. The island name signs moved off the road, Legends Key's too: it stood on the causeway.
  - **Island roads were hidden:** the lawn sat above the ring roads and bridge ends on all three islands. The lawn and sand were lowered.

Missing meshes still to replace: BNW M3 E92 (2 body parts, a door part), a small Nisun 350Z piece and a Panamera piece. The `videoplayback` sound in Workspace is blocked too.

## Main menu, tile style (built 2026-11-03)
- **Built** from `Prompts/Main menu (tile style).md`: `client/MainMenu.lua`, art drawn by `tools/menu_art.py` (ids in `tools/menu_art_ids.json`, module `client/MenuArt.lua`). Our own look: deep navy, warm gold, mint for go. It is the start screen and replaces the old CITY LEGENDS / PLAY screen.
- **Opens from:** the HUD's **MENU** button, which took the SETTINGS button's place (owner, 2026-11-03: "Dont add a keybind instead Replace the settings UI to Menu"). No new key; M still switches the gearbox and the music. While it's open the car is braked and held (`Driving.menuHold`).
- **Top bar:** the car you drive by name, tier chip and colour; **CLAIM CAR** (pulsing gold) for a new player. On the right: headshot, name, level + XP, cash.
- **CAMPAIGN tab:** PLAY (back to driving, or your car out from the start screen; the next Legends Path goal under it), WORLD MAP (live map, time, weather, where you are), ACHIEVEMENTS (cars and Legends Path counts; opens the GOALS screen), SETTINGS.
- **World map:** symbols for every venue, Legends Realty, the races and the delivery dispatches (`shared/TravelSpots.lua`); filters, drag, zoom, You are here. A symbol opens a card with FAST TRAVEL and SET WAYPOINT. Fast travel is the server's (`FastTravel` remote): not while wanted, carrying loot, or in a race / job / duel, 30 s between trips (`Config.Menu.FastTravelCooldown`). The car or you are put down only on solid ground; from the start screen your car comes out there. The waypoint is a mint blip on the minimap plus a light beam; no road GPS line yet.
- **Settings page:** mini map on / off and its corner, graphics Low / Medium / High / Ultra, camera shake, head movement, blur, chase FOV, calm motion, units, gearbox, on-screen controls, music and effects sliders, radio, photo mode, credits. All saved with the profile (`PlayerData.setSettings`).
- **CARS tab:** your cars by brand (sort by brand / price / speed / newest, search, favourites first). Side panel: stats, SPAWN, FAVOURITE, CUSTOMISE (waypoint to Legends Customs), SELL / MARKET. "Get more cars" sets a waypoint to Legends Motors.
- **STORE tab:** VIP, 2x Cash, Starter Pack, Cash $25k, Cash $100k, Garage Space. All greyed "Coming soon": **waiting on the owner's pass / product ids and prices** (`Config.Menu.Store`). The effects written there are suggestions for the owner to confirm. Nothing is sold and there is no ProcessReceipt yet.
- **New player:** profile `Claimed` = false only for a brand new save (old saves count as claimed). The menu opens on "Pick your first car" with 1 of 3: Sompact Swiff "Easy and grippy", Volt Seal EV "Fast but loose", Orient EV6 "Big and comfy" (`Config.Menu.StarterChoices`). The `ClaimStarter` remote is server-checked and once only. In Studio, set the player's attribute `TestNewPlayer` on the server to see it again.

PC and phone-size (667x375) screenshots: `Main menu - PC 1..7`, `Main menu - phone size`.
- **Not tested:** gamepad, a real phone, 2 players.
- **Found (old bug, not fixed):** `Driving.reset` (Backspace, and the fall-out-of-world reset) looks for a road only in the old city. In Monaco it throws the car to the old map's Legends Loop (x 3340). So the menu has no Reset car button.

## Venue remodel (started 2026-11-03, `Prompts/Remodel the venues.md`)
**The plan, in this order (one venue at a time, the owner OKs each before the next):**
1. **Legends Motors (done first, below):** the showroom becomes the ground floor of a cream Belle Époque Riviera block, as in the owner's Monaco photos.
2. **Legends Grand Hotel:**
   - outside: cream stone, iron balconies, a big canopy with gold letters, a red carpet, palms in pots, the valet stand;
   - inside: a lobby with chandeliers and marble, the reception and a lounge.
3. **High-Rollers Casino (scenery only):**
   - outside: a Belle Époque palace with two corner towers, a copper-green roof, an arched glass entrance, gardens and a fountain, floodlit gold at night;
   - inside: a gilded hall and the VIP area.
4. **Legends Reserve Bank:**
   - outside: a stone bank with columns, a bronze door, a carved cornice and flags;
   - inside: a marble banking hall with brass lamps and the round steel vault door. The heist parts are kept.
5. **The 3 towers:**
   - Aurum Residences: a warm-stone Belle Époque high-rise;
   - Skyline One: curved glass;
   - The Crown: slim, with a crown top;
   - each with a marble lobby, the penthouse and a roof pool.
6. **Legends Customs:** a glass and steel workshop with an epoxy floor, 3 lifts, tool walls and a stage-lit TUNING bay.
7. **Gem Showcase:** black stone and bronze, lit display cases.
8. **The rest:** Legends Realty, the chauffeur kiosks, the challenge gantries.

Each venue uses the place's own dressing and cleaned Toolbox models (no scripts, no logos).

**Legends Motors, pass 1 (2026-11-03):**
- **How it's built:** `tools/dealer_remodel.lua` (run in Studio as `ServerStorage.RivieraTools.DealerRemodel`) edits `ServerStorage.DealerBody`.
  - All new parts are in `DealerBody.Remodel`.
  - Recoloured parts keep their old look in `Orig*` attributes; `.undo(body)` puts them back.
  - The untouched copy is `ServerStorage.DealerBody_before_remodel`.
- **Outside:**
  - 5 floors of cream stone above the showroom, with window bays, carved hoods and keystones;
  - stone balconies with black iron railings on floors 2 and 4, and a long iron balcony on the top floor;
  - side windows, a big cornice, a slate mansard roof with dormers, and chimneys;
  - a light-grey concrete band over the glass with big gold serif letters LEGENDS MOTORS (self-lit, not neon);
  - a black awning with a gold line and LEGENDS MOTORS · RIVIERA;
  - a light stone base with metal vents, and warm floodlights on the facade;
  - out front: light stone paving, black bollards with red bands, white stone cubes, and wooden planters with palms (the map's own pieces);
  - the outside walls are light stone.
- **Inside:**
  - a pale terrazzo floor and a white ceiling;
  - 7 black light tracks on the diagonal with small warm spots (14 real lights);
  - a wood-slat back wall with gold letters (it sits behind the old model's big sea-picture wall, so it's mostly hidden);
  - beige curtains along one side;
  - the indoor display cars turned to angles (±24°).
- **Gameplay not touched:** the prompts, the desk, the drive-out bay and the car spots stay the same. Tested in Play: it builds and the counter is still there. Buying and test driving were not re-tested this pass.
- **Numbers:** the live venue went from about 1,500 to 2,500 parts. FPS was not measured. Before / after pictures: `Legends Motors remodel - before and after.jpg`.
- **Still to do here:**
  - the display cars show as the old blocky built cars in Studio tests (the showcase skinning didn't run there);
  - a lounge (sofa, coffee machine);
  - real Toolbox furniture;
  - the neighbour café with its awning;
  - the garage ramp;
  - a phone-size screenshot;
  - a 2-player test.
- **The owner must save the place** (Ctrl+S): the remodel lives in the place, not in the scripts.

**Legends Grand Hotel, pass 1 (2026-11-03):**
- **How it's built:** the hotel is built by script at start (`server/Hotel.lua`), so this pass is in the code, not in the place. The ready-made pieces come from `ServerStorage.VenueKit` (saved in the place): without it the hotel uses its own built pieces.
- **Outside:**
  - the tower is now cream Riviera stone (was dark glass with pink and teal neon), with punched windows, black iron balconies on every other floor, carved cornices with stone balustrades at both setbacks, and LEGENDS GRAND HOTEL in gold letters round the top;
  - the crown: stone corner piers with gold lanterns and red aviation lights, stone rings round the glass suites;
  - the podium: tall lobby glass between stone piers, dark bronze frames, a stone band with an iron balconette, a stone door frame with a keystone, a carved cornice and a stone balustrade on the roof;
  - the wings: windows with stone hoods and iron balconettes, the boutiques (ATELIER, THE SPA) with dark glass lit warm from inside and black awnings with gold names, VALET GARAGE in gold;
  - the entrance: an iron-and-glass marquise with a gold-lettered black fascia, iron columns with gold collars, glass lanterns, brass bollards, the red carpet, potted palms by the doors, a brass luggage cart;
  - the forecourt: the map's own palms, the fountain lit warm;
  - the vertical pink HOTEL sign and every neon strip are gone (only warm lamp glass and the red aviation lights glow).
  - The tower's stone body, window strips and name band always stream to every player (`TowerShell`, about 250 parts), so it shows from far away.
- **Inside the lobby:** 3 crystal chandeliers, tufted leather sofas and armchairs in the lounge, potted palms, a luggage cart; gold strips are brass now.
- **Not changed:** the suites (they are built by the towers' code: done with the towers), the valet bay, stand, elevators, suite sales and the residents' board.
- **Tested in Play:** it builds, 50 prompts as before; valet: car handed over in the bay ("The valet is parking your ...") and brought back to the bay. The elevators were not ridden this pass (their code is unchanged).
- **Numbers:** about 4,060 parts (was about 2,440); the 4 suites are 1,370 of them. FPS not measured. Pictures: [[Legends Grand Hotel remodel - before and after.jpg]].
- **Still to do here:** a phone-size check, a 2-player test.

**Hotel suites: one suite you upgrade (2026-11-03, owner: "the 4 suites will be in the same location but when u upgrade it in lobby the inside of the suites will change and the highest will be pent house"; "make the interior of the suites a million times better, insert some items from toolbox"):**
- You own one suite at a time: Skyline → Ocean → Royal → **Penthouse** (the top tier, was "Presidential Suite"; id `grand_presidential` kept).
- **Buying:** the lobby's SUITE UPGRADES plinths. The first buys the Skyline ($2M); each next one upgrades, costing the difference ($4M, $6M, $13M: $25M in all, as before). Prompts say "Upgrade", "Your suite" or "Locked: upgrade to the X first".
- **Lifts:** 3 lifts marked SUITES, "Up to my suite": only owners go up, and only to their own tier. The roof (pool, helipad) is the Penthouse's only.
- **Income:** only your current tier pays (Skyline $2,000/min ... Penthouse $30,000/min). Old saves that bought several suites keep only the best one (its home and showcase car move to it).
- **How it works:** the 4 tiers are still 4 floors at the top of the tower, but you only ever ride to yours, so to the player it is one suite that changes.
- **Inside, by tier:**
  - all: lounge chairs, a wood and marble kitchen island, plants, a rug under the dining table;
  - Skyline: oak floor, white corner sofa, hotel bed, dining table by the window;
  - Ocean: + car turntable, leather corner sofa, dining table in the middle;
  - Royal: + DJ booth and party mode, cream marble floor, glass fireplace, tufted sofa and armchairs facing the view, a chandelier;
  - Penthouse: black marble floor, round bed, freestanding bath, glass dining table, concert grand piano by the window, two chandeliers, palms, the roof.
- TV ticker no longer runs outside the red bar.
- **Tested in Play:** buy, the upgrade order, not enough cash, the lift for non-owners and owners, the roof (owner only), another tier's floor (refused), prompt wording.
- **Numbers:** the suites' furniture is about 1,100 parts on all 4 floors (pieces merged; the piano went from 4,599 to 74 parts). FPS not measured.

## Aventador SV exhaust (2026-11-03)
- **Owner:** "find and add better exhaust sounds for Aventador SV", then "the Aventador rev doesn't sound good", then "it still sounds bad, use different rpm for each sound Low, Mid, High".
- Each sound was checked in Studio: it loads, its length, its loudness, and its engine speed from its firing note (a V12 sounds at rpm / 10 Hz).
- **Now:** the Aventador's own L539 V12, recorded on the throttle at set revs (uploader ItzDeadsecc, public):
  - Low = "l539-eng-acc-2000" 119081006371451 (2,000 rpm)
  - Mid = "l539-eng-acc-4000" 82866280346883 (4,000 rpm)
  - High = "l539-eng-acc-6500" 138629257229746 (6,500 rpm)
  - Idle = the real SV's idle "Lamborghini_Aventador_idle" 73235710388670 (Takumi112892).
  - Each loop is pitched at most about a third off; one band at a time with a short crossfade (`Config.ExhaustBands.lambrini_lp750`: Idle 1,020 / 2,000 / 4,000 / 6,500, `width` 0.28).
  - Lift-off pops "Aventador SVJ Crackle4", upshift crack "Crackle1", the SVJ's shift / downshift; start-up, launch, crackle and the owner's limiter kept.
- **Tried and dropped the same day:** the "Aventador SVJ Revloop" bank (1,000 / 4,198 / 8,375 rpm: a gap right where you pull away), then one ~4,700 rpm loop across all the revs.
- `Audio.lua`: near idle and off the throttle only the idle loop plays; `gain` / `gains` level loops recorded louder or softer; `width` sets how far each band reaches.
- Checked in Play: idle, pulling away (Low), the pull through the gears (Mid, then High), lift-off; no errors. Not heard here (no sound): the owner listens.
- Same bank, if wanted: on-throttle 1,250 / 3,000 / 3,500 / 5,200 / 8,700 rpm and off-throttle 3,000 / 3,500 / 4,500 / 5,000 / 6,200 / 8,700 rpm (search "l539-eng"). Another Low / Mid / High set: "l539vtwelvelowrev / midrev / highrev" (YaBoyZenvo).
- **Then (owner, same day): "use the SVJ exhaust sound and share it with the SV, but make the SV 2x quieter; and on the SVJ the downshift sound doesn't match the actual downshift":**
  - The SV now plays the SVJ's whole exhaust set (copied in `Config.lua`) at half volume (`Config.ExhaustVolume.lambrini_lp750 = 0.5`: every loop, the start-up and each one-shot). The L539 Low / Mid / High set above is out (ids kept in the comment).
  - Downshift: the shift sounds used to wait for the gear number on the dash, which changes only after the shift is done, while the revs had already moved. Now `Driving` plays them the moment the gearbox takes the new gear (the gear the revs follow), and the downshift blip is pitched to the revs the car lands on (`Config.ExhaustShotRpm`: the SVJ's blip settles at ~4,200 rpm), with a short gap so quick double downshifts don't stack. Every car with shift sounds gets this.
  - Checked in Play on a straight (Coast Road): SV idle at half the SVJ's loudness; braking from 72 mph, three downshifts at 41 / 35 / 27 mph, each blip at its own pitch (0.85 / 0.90 / 1.00); no errors.

## Aventador LP700-4 exhaust (2026-11-05, in Studio, not published)
- **Owner:** "can u replace the lp 700 exhaust sound with the SV's exhaust but make it 1.6 times quieter and replace the start up with sound: 6718630582 also make the exhaust sound realistic".
- **Before:** the LP700 had no exhaust of its own (the shared engine loop).
- **Now:** the SV's loops and one-shots (`Config.lua`), at 0.5 / 1.6 = 0.3125 (`Config.ExhaustVolume`), so every loop and one-shot is the SV's level divided by 1.6. Start-up: "Lamborghini Aventador SVJ Startup" 6718630582 (1Rhymastic, public), scaled the same way (volume 0.297; to change only it, edit `crank` in `Audio.lua`).
- **Measured in Studio** (`AudioPlayer:GetWaveformAsync`, then the firing note: a V12 fires at rpm / 10 Hz): Idle 100 Hz = 1,000 rpm, Low 502 Hz = 5,020 rpm (its lines are 41.7 Hz apart, which is half the engine's turning speed at 5,000; not a 2,500 rpm loop), Mid 600 Hz = 6,000 rpm, High ~790 Hz = 7,900 rpm, Launch 504 Hz = 5,040 rpm, limiter 820 Hz = 8,200 rpm.
- **Why the SV's mapping wasn't kept:** it pitches by fixed rev points, so at 4,000 rpm one loop played ~3,400 and the next ~5,200 at once (two notes), and the low band sounded like 5,000 rpm with the needle at 2,700. SV and SVJ are unchanged.
- **Realistic, LP700 only (`Config.ExhaustBands.lambrini_lp700`, `Config.ExhaustStart`):**
  - Each loop is pitched from its measured rpm, so all loops play the same note and it follows the rev counter. Checked in Play: Low, Mid and High report the same rpm in a full-throttle pull and a neutral rev.
  - Nothing is recorded between 1,000 and 5,000 rpm: the idle reaches up to 2,200 rpm and Low reaches down to it.
  - Lifting off: quieter and darker (EQ mid -3 dB, high -12 dB). Upshift: the note drops about 50% for ~0.1 s (ignition cut). Limiter and launch loops pitched to the real revs.
  - Start-up: the clip's engine catches at 0.8 s, the same moment as `Driving`'s crank. It ends at ~1,340 rpm, so the idle comes in there and settles to 1,020 rpm over 2.5 s, with the clip faded out (it ends abruptly). Touching the throttle cuts the clip short and the engine answers at once. The clip is loaded when you get in (it took 0.45 s to start the first time otherwise).
- **Tested in Play (on a test save, `CityLegends_soundtest`, real store name put back):** start-up, idle settle, throttle during start-up, full-throttle pull with shifts, neutral rev to the limiter, launch hold, lift-off; the SV still on its own path; no new errors. Volumes: idle 0.16 (SV 0.26), Low 0.50 (SV 0.81), High 0.30 (SV 0.48), one-shots 0.25-0.27. **Not heard: Claude has no sound here, the owner listens.**
- **Pops and bangs removed (same day, in Studio, not published):** owner: "remove the pop and bangs on the lp700". LP700 only (`noPops` in `Config.ExhaustBands`):
  - Gone: the SV's Pops and ShiftPop clips, the shared `Audio.backfire`, and the exhaust flames that go with pops (`exhaustPop`, on hard upshifts and lift-offs). Nitro flames stay.
  - Also gone: the SV's Shift clip. It holds a bang at 0.40-0.48 s, 5 times the clip's level and louder than the Pops clip, and it played every ~0.1 s on a lift as the auto box ran up the gears. The upshift is now the note dropping (`shiftCut`), with no clip of its own. The owner may want a clean shift clip here.
  - Checked for bangs and kept: Downshift, Idle, Low, Mid, High, Launch, Redline, start-up (the limiter and launch clips stutter by nature).
  - Tested in Play on the same road (`CityLegends_soundtest`, real store name put back), 4 pulls to ~107 mph with a lift each. SV: Shift clip 20, ShiftPop 7, Pops 4, backfire 7, flames 18. LP700: 0 of each. No new errors. **Not heard.**
