- **Choices** are listed just below. Everything changed in the place (marina, road fixes, house bodies) has a backup or an undo noted in its step.

### Choices made while the owner was away (2026-11-03)
- Buildings: the global environment reflections (`Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale` 1) were left as they are. Lowering them takes the slight sky sheen off the walls but also dulls the cars' paint and the wet roads, and the sheen wasn't the black / white flashing. Easy to change in `CityBuilder` / Lighting if wanted.
- Buildings: trims, piers and the dark foot band sit 0.05 studs in front of the wall they overlap (not visible as a step). Each moved part has an `Unstacked` attribute; `tools/riviera/Unstack.luau` `undo` puts them back.
- Smooth roads: no Blender curved-mesh rebuild. The road surface is already continuous triangles that follow the curves (wedge pairs from `Roads.luau`), so there are no saw teeth left on the asphalt. A mesh rebuild needs Import 3D by hand in Studio and would throw away the junction, paint, kerb and island fixes made in the place. Instead every lane was tested and fixed in the place (step 1.2).
- Smooth roads: the checker still counts 1,759 small kinks over 0.05 studs (1,094 under 0.1, 396 at 0.1-0.2, 234 at 0.2-0.3, 35 at 0.3-0.4, none bigger). Most are where two road pieces meet at a junction (the side road sits 0.05 lower on purpose so it doesn't flicker) or where the slope changes between road samples. Smoothing all of them means solving the heights again and rebuilding every road (losing the in-place fixes), so they were left.
- Smooth roads: the 163 speed bumps the lane test finds were left: they are meant to be there.
- Smooth roads: `src/shared/RivieraLanes.lua` is no longer exactly what `export_lanes.py` writes: 134 lane points were moved off kerbs and walls. The moves are in `tools/map_plan/lane_nudges.json`; after a new export run `tools/map_plan/apply_lane_nudges.py`.
- Dealer menu: gold is the main-action and selection colour (BUY, the picked brand / trim / row), the same gold as the main menu, with mint-green prices and blue for owned cars. The old red accent and the red band in the showroom are gone (the room's line is warm white now). Panels are dark glass drawn with gradients and a thin light edge: Roblox can't blur only behind a panel (a Blur effect would blur the car too).
- Dealer menu: the floor is dark and only slightly shiny (Reflectance 0.05). Roblox reflections show the sky, not the room, so a mirror floor turned pale blue by day.
- Dealer menu: phones (and any screen under 1000 x 520) get a compact layout: the same parts, bigger type, 46 px buttons, no level box (the level is still in the HUD), the speed bar shows km/h only.
- Dealer menu: Esc closes it (also when Roblox's menu takes the key). Backspace doesn't: the driving keys saw it too and put the car back on the road.
- Venues: the bank is remodelled in its code (`server/Bank.lua`, `Bank.dress`), like the hotel was, not with a Toolbox building: the bank is built at start and its vault, prompts and carts are placed by that code. Its furniture comes from the cleaned kit already in the place (`ServerStorage.VenueKit`: chandeliers, Chesterfield sofa, armchair, potted palms). Walls are Sandstone (Limestone looked white in the sun), trims and columns cream Limestone.
- Venues, the 3 towers: remodelled outside only (code, `Towers.dress` / `Towers.noNeon` in `server/Towers.lua`). Their penthouses and roofs were already redone with the hotel suites (same code), and their glass body, lobby and gameplay stay. The towers' coloured neon (cyan, purple and gold strips, the glowing pool rim, the lobby's neon chandelier rings) became gold metal or warm lit glass, except the DJ booth's party lights (they need to glow) and the windows' night glow (every building has it).
- Venues, Gem Showcase: the small gems inside the cases and windows keep their glow (they are the products, a stud or two big, and read as jewels only that way), and so do the red security lasers round the Legends Heart. The big gems outside (over the pool, the roof diamond) are glass and bronze now, floodlit warm.
- Venues: Legends Rewards keeps its warm white glow strips (coves, sconces, lanterns, downlights: 62 parts). They are warm white light, which the rule allows; only coloured neon was taken out elsewhere.
- Map realism (step 4): the three realism prompts were already worked through (lighting per time of day, skies, buildings v2 with recessed windows and Monaco colours, the far city, Toolbox props and trees, the speed pass). The one item left, a PBR material kit (MaterialVariants for plaster, stone and marble on the buildings), was not done this run: it changes the look of every building at once and needs texture images; better with the owner's eye on it. Only 4 variants exist (carbon, 3 pavers).
- Houses (step 5): the Creator Store search turned up few houses good enough (most results are old blocky builds or Brookhaven copies with fake "verification" pop-ups and scripts). Two were good enough after cleaning and were added as bodies; the other Low / Mid designs keep their built-here houses until better models turn up. The 11 bodies already in the place (2 Mid, 9 High) stay.
- Marina parking: built on the harbour's east quay, between the Coast Road and the water, because the road runs flat at the quay's level there, so the drive in is almost level (no big ramp). A Belle Epoque block already stands on that quay; it was kept and the car park wraps round it on the water side.
- Marina parking: the parked supercars come from the game's own parked-car system (`Parking.lua`: 7 High-tier cars in random bays, they can be stolen like the garage ones and come back after a while). Yachts are left for the owner: every berth has an invisible `Berth` part to drop a yacht model onto.
- Order: step 7 (the HOUSES tab) was done before step 6.1 (the mountain highway), see the summary. The highway's route was surveyed: from the Mountain Road's west end (x 11120, z -176, height ~215) west and down to the plateau (x 9500-10500, z -600 to -2400, heights 90-180: room for a long straight), then sweeping bends up into the mountains north of the Mountain Road (heights 400-700) and back down to join it again around x 12700, z -640.
- Main menu: the HOUSES tab reuses the house screens players already have (HousingClient's catalogue and house menu) instead of new copies, so there's one place to change them. On the start screen (no car yet) a house button brings your car out first.

### Step 1.1: buildings flashing black and white (done)
- **Cause:** the far stand-ins (`ReplicatedStorage.RivieraFar`, `tools/riviera/FarCity.luau`) show past ~950 studs (570 on Low graphics, less on phones) and while a building is streamed out. Each one's pale wall-coloured shell was sized from the building's outer skin, so it stood 0.2 studs IN FRONT of the dark core inside every window and shopfront. Close up (stand-in hidden) the windows were dark; past the range (stand-in shown) every window and shop turned the wall colour. That's the "black from one spot, white from another". Pictures: [[Buildings flashing - before (far stand-in showing).jpg]], [[Buildings flashing - after.jpg]], [[Buildings flashing - after, far stand-ins over the city.jpg]].
- **Fixed:** all 645 stand-in shells now sit inside the dark core (0.4 inside its faces), in the place and in `FarCity.luau`. 5 stand-ins of buildings that were deleted (ghost buildings that appeared far away) removed. Backup: `ServerStorage.RivieraFar_backup_2026-11-03`.
- **Also fixed:** faces on one plane (they flicker by angle): 20,074 pairs with a different colour or material (the dark foot band over the piers, stone sills sunk in bands, door / shop glass over the foot, roofs / parapets / plant rooms) plus 221,385 same-colour pairs (pier over band: their textures were offset). New `tools/riviera/Unstack.luau` pushes the front part 0.05 out; run on all 605 buildings and the Old Rock (53,504 parts moved; about 800 pairs left, each a tiny corner where two pushes meet). `Buildings.luau` runs it after every build now.
- **Checked:** other causes: walls are Plaster / Concrete / Limestone (no reflectance, no shiny walls), no meshes with flipped faces on the buildings (only café furniture), the only half-see-through textures are café tables, the lit-window glow is night only. Play: no errors, 74 fps, 417 stand-ins shown far away with dark windows. Not done here: a full drive at night on a phone (do on the next playtest).

### Step 1.2: smooth roads (done)
- **Lane test:** new `tools/riviera/LaneCheck.luau` walks all 144 lanes (2 each way on every road) a stud at a time, in the middle and 3 studs to each side (a car's width): 207,674 points. It logs holes, kerb / pavement / wall under the lane, anything standing in the lane, and height kinks.
- **Before → after:** holes 15 → 0. Kerb, pavement or wall under a lane 72 → 0. Things standing in a lane 39 → 0 (plus the 163 speed bumps, kept). Ledges 0.5-0.9 studs 3 → 0; nothing over 0.4 is left (see the choices above).
- **Fixed:**
  - 15 holes filled with asphalt patches (`tools/riviera/HoleFill.luau`, attribute `HoleFill`), the pit at the West Switchbacks, and ground poking through the road at the top of the West Switchbacks.
  - 40 kerb and pavement pieces lying on the asphalt inside junctions removed (backup `ServerStorage.RoadPiecesRemoved_2026-11-04`), plus 6 stray edge lines.
  - 134 lane points moved 0.5-5 studs off kerbs and walls (traffic now keeps its whole width on the asphalt).
  - Stadium (Riviera Bay): its walls stood on the road at the corner where the road bends. The corner is cut back (8 studs each way plus a short angled wall; old place in the attributes `OldCFrame` / `OldSize`) and the floodlight mast moved in. [[Smooth roads - stadium corner in the lane, before.jpg]], [[Smooth roads - stadium corner, after.jpg]].
  - 2 real ledges ramped out with the new `tools/riviera/Reshape.luau` (moves road triangle corners and rebuilds them; the lines move with the road): the end of the West Switchbacks (a 0.6-0.9 hump in the junction) and Casino Avenue / East Climb 3 (the west side came in 0.9 above the junction). Backup `ServerStorage.ReshapeBackup` (`Reshape.undo()` puts it all back). [[Smooth roads - West Switchbacks end, before (ledge).jpg]], [[Smooth roads - West Switchbacks end, after.jpg]], [[Smooth roads - Casino Avenue junction, before (ledge).jpg]], [[Smooth roads - Casino Avenue junction, after.jpg]].
- **Checked:** Play: no script errors, 28 traffic cars driving, 80 fps (74 at step 1.1). `luau-lsp analyze` clean. Thin seam lines between road triangles still show in a few junctions (they were there before; they're not bumps). Not done here: a drive of every road with two cars at high speed (do on the next playtest).

- **Fixed:** with the Analogue dial, the GET OUT pill covered the new car strip (class badge + car level, added with car levels). The pill now sits 28 px higher on PC (`UI.setAction`), clear of the strip. [[Speedometer check - analogue, Get out over the car strip (before).jpg]], [[Speedometer check - analogue, Get out moved up (after).jpg]]. Phones are unchanged (the pill is on the right, the dial in the middle).

The Output shows a warning for a Sound named `videoplayback` in Workspace (`rbxassetid://109026241087535`: "Asset is not approved for the requester"): an audio in the place that Roblox hasn't approved; harmless, it just doesn't play. Not checked: phone size (no touch emulator through the MCP).

### Step 2: driving feel check (done, nothing broken)
Driven in Play on the Coast Road (automatic, full throttle, then full brake, then a steer at 60-80 km/h), timed by a script:

| Car | 0-100 km/h | Speed after 9 s | Full brake | Steer (yaw, stays upright) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Sompact Swiff (light hatch) | 0-60 in 5.2 s | 88 km/h | from 88 km/h: 21 m | OK |
| Bavarica Werkz 540i (sedan) | 5.2 s (real ~5.0) | 140 km/h | from 109 km/h: 34 m | 53°/s, OK |
| Apex Land Cruzer 200 (SUV) | 7.9 s (real ~8) | 110 km/h | from 110 km/h: 41 m | 43°/s, OK |
| Enfurecido Aventador SVJ (hypercar) | 3.0 s (real 2.8) | 211 km/h | from 211 km/h: 93 m | 48°/s, no slide, OK |

- No flips, no shaking, gears change, the cars stay flat in the steer test. No script errors.
- Not changed (not bugs): the brakes stop at about 1.2-1.7 g (a real road car does about 1 g): that's on purpose, 1.5 x real since 2026-11-03 (`Config.DrivingFeel.ClassFeel` brake). The Swiff (retired) still has a blocky part-built model in `CarModels`.

**Legends Reserve Bank (done, 2026-11-04):**
- **Outside:** warm sandstone walls with cream stone trims; a rusticated base with deep joints and a band; the six columns with moulded rings and scroll capitals; a carved entablature (architrave, dentils, a deep two-step cornice); a pediment over the middle with a gold medallion; a stone balustrade along the roof; two flagpoles with the bank's own navy-and-gold flag; tall dark windows with stone hoods, keystones and bronze grilles beside the doors; the three doorways with open bronze door leaves (gold handles) and bronze grilles over them; side windows with hoods, sills, keystones and iron grilles, pilasters between them; stone cheek blocks at the stair with iron lantern posts; clipped box trees in stone planters. At night: warm uplights at every column, the lanterns and the old floodlights (all only at night).
- **Inside:** 6 crystal chandeliers (kit; the old ones had a neon ring); a coffered ceiling; wood panelling with a brass rail and marble pilasters; a dark marble border and a red runner to the counter; the teller counter with raised wood panels, a brass rail and brass bankers' lamps with green glass shades; a queue line of brass posts and velvet ropes; a big clock; the vault with riveted steel cladding, a VAULT plate and yellow-black stripes on the floor; a guard desk with 3 screens; 4 CCTV cameras; a waiting corner with a Chesterfield sofa, an armchair and palms. No neon left in the bank.

Bright purple / green tiles, white pills and arrows are gone. Pictures: [[Main menu - before restyle.jpg]], [[Main menu - new look, CAMPAIGN.jpg]], [[Main menu - new look, CARS.jpg]], [[Main menu - new look, HOUSES.jpg]], [[Main menu - new look, STORE.jpg]], [[Main menu - new look, WORLD MAP.jpg]].

## Traffic pile-up at the Mountain Road hairpin (fixed 2026-11-04)
Owner: "the cars are bugging here" ([[Traffic pile-up at the Mountain Road hairpin (owner).png]]: traffic cars stacked on each other where the Mountain Road turns down into the West Switchbacks). Four causes, all fixed:
- **Dead ends (26 places):** a turn that leaves a lane a piece early (the clean-arc turns of 2026-11-03) left the lane's last piece going nowhere; cars driving straight on hit the end, jumped back into the car behind and piled up. 6 more lanes redrawn by hand kept their junction links on the old last point (Bay Link, West Switchbacks, Casino Avenue, East Climbs 1 and 4). Now `RivieraTraffic` moves such links to the lane's real end and takes every piece with no way on out of the graph (0 left); a car that still reaches an end waits and is moved out of sight.
- **Side by side:** where a road narrows its two lanes run closer than a car's width (58 spots); two cars side by side each saw the other just ahead and both stopped for good. A car going the same way that isn't ahead of your front half now counts as beside you; a car already overlapping the one ahead can be let through after 6 s.
- **Height:** distances to you were measured including height, so on the mountain (215 up) cars that turned off the yellow route were whisked away right in front of you, over and over.
- **Same height bug, wider:** cut-ups (near misses) never counted above ~45 studs (most of the Riviera), traffic didn't wait for players at junctions up there, and a car brought out on a hill road didn't clear the traffic around it. All measure at the right height now.
- Tested in Play: 90 s at the hairpin: 26 cars went down the switchback (0 before), no cars stacked, longest wait 6.5 s (it was minutes). Mountain Road drive: 3 cut-ups counted at heights 120-215. Bay Link, East Climb 1 / Coast Road: no stacking or standstills.

- **The 3 yellow routes** (Coast Road, Mountain Road, Town Road): unchanged, 2 + 2 lanes.
- **The 33 other streets:** one lane each way. Their lane lines (the dashes between lane 1 and 2, 4,161 pieces) are taken off; the centre dashes and the edge lines stay. ## Turning at the Mountain Road end (fixed 2026-11-04)
Owner: "there is a traffic problem here, its because they dont know how to turn properly" ([[Traffic queue at the Mountain Road end (owner).png]]: a long queue where the Mountain Road turns down into the West Switchbacks).
- **Why:** with the switchbacks now one lane each way, both Mountain Road lanes turned into that one lane at the very end, cutting across each other, and the inner lane had two turns side by side (a sharp one and a gentle one) that made cars wait for each other. Coming up, the switchbacks fed the Mountain Road's two lanes through a very tight turn into the outer one.
- **Now** (`RivieraTraffic`): where a yellow route ends into a street, its outer lane merges into the inner one about 200 studs before the end (a lane change; merging cars give way to the lane they move into), and only the inner lane turns, with one gentle turn. At a route's start, the street feeds the inner lane and cars move into the outer lane a little further on. One turn per lane end and target lane everywhere. Lane changes don't slow down like turns.
- **Also:** a car right in front in the same lane is never ignored any more (two cars could drive on inside each other).
- Tested in Play at the junction: the queue on the approach is 4-9 cars instead of a standstill, 26-28 cars a minute and a bit go down the switchbacks, no cars stacked (1 brief overlap at the merge in 70 s, was 92), longest wait 5.5 s (a car waiting for a gap to merge). Turn paths checked with the lane test against the road: no new kerb or hole spots. A stricter "balanced arc" turn search was tried and switched off (`STRICT_TURNS`): it made 35 more turn paths cross kerbs.
- **Turns over kerbs (same day):** `tools/riviera/TurnPicks.luau` checks every junction turn's path a car's width wide and, where it ran onto a kerb or pavement, picks other pieces to leave / join so the arc stays on the road (`src/shared/RivieraTurnPicks.lua`, 11 picks). 5 turns still graze a kerb with a wheel (Climbs 1 / 3 / 5, Casino Climb, East Climb 3 onto the Coast Road: tight kerb corners at those mouths); those need the junction kerbs reshaped (with the curved road meshes).

## Riviera Highway and the Summit Pass (2026-11-04, owner-away step 6.1 / 6.2 / 6.4)
Owner (while it was being built): "Make the highway roads like this" (Stelvio-pass photos: stacked hairpins; a rock arch over a cliff road), "make the summit roads lead to these roads which will make a long loop and then arrive back at the roads of monaco", "if you already finish the long highway dont abandon it, just make it lead to these tight curves 1+1 lane roads", "quality over quantity". Map: [[Riviera Highway - map.png]].
- **The loop you can drive:** town → Mountain Road (the summit road) → **Summit Pass** (1+1, tight hairpins) → **Riviera Highway** (2+2) → back into town on the West Switchbacks. The highway also has its own **Highway Loop** in the north valley.
- **Riviera Highway** (2+2, gold on the map, 2,320 studs): leaves the West Switchbacks' lower west hairpin (it carries straight on out of the bend) and runs up the west valley, a ~1,600-stud nearly straight run, to a T with the Highway Loop (3,252 studs, sweeping bends round a low hill and a ~900-stud straight up the valley floor). Grades at most 8.5%, concrete shoulders, steel crash barriers both sides (2,512 parts), 168 umbrella pines on the banks.
- **Summit Pass** (1+1, 5,328 studs): from a T on the highway's valley road east to the peak's west face, up a stack of 4 legs and 3 hairpins (Stelvio-style, legs 71 studs apart), along a cliff above the Mountain Road with the sea below and through a **rock arch** (rock pillars on the cliff side with openings, like the owner's photo), then down a stack of 3 legs and 2 hairpins on the south face, cut into the rock, to a T on the Mountain Road. Grades at most 10%, eased to flat at both joins; low cream stone parapets along both edges that lean with the road (the first walls were stepped and their tops flickered: owner, "the side is glitching out a little"). The shape was searched by `tools/map_plan/pass_fit.py` (least digging: average cut 48 → 29 studs). Pictures: [[Summit Pass - rock arch.jpg]], [[Summit Pass - cliff road above the Mountain Road.jpg]], [[Summit Pass - hairpin leg.jpg]], [[Summit Pass - the stack from the Mountain Road.jpg]].
- **Ground:** cut and filled along the roads (tools/map_plan/highway_terrain.py; pale rock cuttings, earth embankments, the rest untouched); the first try (from the Mountain Road's west end) was put back because that junction is the hairpin the next prompt rebuilds.
- **Traffic:** lanes added (`src/shared/RivieraLanesHighway.lua`: Studio refuses a script over 200,000 characters, so they're a second module that RivieraTraffic joins on), at most 10 traffic cars on the highway and pass (`Config.Traffic.RivieraHighwayMax`; seen: exactly 10). The pass is one lane each way in traffic.
- **Also:** fast travel **Riviera Highway** (the valley straight), a race **Riviera Highway** (5,518 studs: the highway, once round the loop), the minimap redrawn (it now reaches 560 studs further west; the marina car park is on it too).
- **Checked:** the lane test on every highway and pass lane: 0 holes, 0 kerbs / walls, 0 things in the lane, biggest step 0.23. A test car drove the whole highway, the loop and the whole pass (100 s, up to 345 and down). The junction mouths: the pass's surface was bent to meet the highway's and the Mountain Road's (Reshape), gaps patched (HoleFill). Map pieces moved out of the way are in `ServerStorage.HighwayHidden` (each with WasIn).
- **Not done yet:** yachts, lamps on the pass. The south stack is cut deep into the steep face (a big grey rock bowl seen from the Mountain Road): the face is too steep and the Mountain Road too close below for the legs to sit on the slope like the Stelvio's.

## Building materials (2026-11-04)
The PBR material kit left from the map realism step. Five MaterialVariants made with Studio's material generator (each came with 3 spare versions, in `MaterialService`): RivStucco (Plaster), RivLimestone (Limestone), RivConcrete2 (Concrete), RivSlate (Slate), RivClayTile (ClayRoofTiles); walls tile every 22 studs. They're **base material overrides**, so every part of those materials changed at once (148,000 building parts plus kerbs, parapets, viaducts) and no part was edited. **Undo one in a line** (Studio command bar): `game:GetService("MaterialService"):SetBaseMaterialOverride(Enum.Material.Plaster, "")`.

## The ground under the plates (2026-11-04, The Crown)
Owner: "At the crown building, its floating and the floor is bugging can u find all the bugs and fix".
- **What was wrong:** later ground writes (the highway, the north roads) put the old hillside back round the stone plates in `Riviera.Sites`, which `Sites.luau` had levelled. The Crown's west half stood 12 studs in the air and the hill came up through 18% of its floor. Same bug elsewhere: lots `riv_low_17` to `20` were buried under the hill, `riv_high_2` and `3` hung up to 66 studs in the air, and the casino, Gem Showcase and 11 other lots had hill poking through at an edge.
- **Fix:** `tools/riviera/SitePads.luau` rewrites only terrain columns: ground 4.4 under each plate, an earth bank down or a cutting up round it, roads and buildings left alone. Neighbouring plates are solved together (`group`, `tiles`, `fixTile`), or one lot's bank buries the next. Done on 47 plates. The 20 plates that had the problem also got a stone retaining wall (`SiteWall`, children of the plate). [[The Crown - lot on its bank.jpg]] [[House lots - ground fixed.jpg]]
- **Checked:** 0 plates with ground through them. Whole-map lane test: only the speed bumps in the lanes, no road buried. A car drove The Crown's ramp and across its forecourt (flat, no bump), and both casino ramps again. No garage or anything else under The Crown. Props round it all stand on the ground.
- **Also fixed at The Crown:** the lobby floor's two marble inlays were 0.01 apart and flickered (in all three towers, `Towers.lua`). One small gap in the road on Terrace W1 patched.
- **After any new terrain run:** run `SitePads` again, because ground writes can bring this back.

## Bug hunt (2026-11-04)
Owner: "find bugs that would annoy players and can u fix it all". Five code audits, then fixes, then Play tests.
- **Saves (worst):** one failed load gave a blank profile and the next autosave wiped the real save. Now 4 tries, then a calm kick, never a save. Session lock (`Session` field, UpdateAsync; a crashed server's lock runs out after 180 s, so that player may be asked to rejoin for up to 3 minutes). `BindToClose` waits for saves. Reward cash can't be lost when leaving mid-claim. The free car no longer comes back on rejoin.
- **Cars:** TEST DRIVE left you with no car (fixed, also the 5-minute swap). Car rolled on after GET OUT. Sea: back on the road after 2 s. Double tap no longer makes two cars. Slow first spawn no longer strands you. Refused test drive no longer moves your next spawn. Wanted timer no longer freezes (`MinChaseSeconds` 8). Manual is forced to Auto on phones (no Q / E there).
- **Houses:** 27 of 45 lots had a 2-3.6 stud step off the road: `EntranceRamp` on every lot now. High underground garages were sealed by the stone plate (plate off while the house stands). Lifts wait for streaming. "Set as home" works from the no-house panel. Drive car faces the gate. Guests can't be trapped. TELEPORT HOME has the fast travel checks. Roommates get the mailbox menu. Emptied bays stay empty. Old "Legends Isle" text gone, CLAIM A LOT sets a waypoint. Helicopter tour no longer flies to the empty sea.
- **Activities:** duels can start (finish from race checkpoints). Lamps and trees can be knocked down again (`Riviera.Dressing` only; palms can't, they have no trunk part). Chips no longer vanish unpaid. A solo racer no longer locks a ring. Casino Avenue start faces the road. First drift no longer capped. Jump goals hidden: the map has no ramps yet.
- **Phone UI:** seven side buttons in rows of three; tracker, rewards pill and daily card off while driving (they lay on the pedals); pedals off on foot; RESET CAR and Camera view in Settings, Leaderboard in MORE; toasts show over the menu; My Cars scaled; start screen tiles no longer leave you stuck; photo mode exit; rewards page no longer wipes typing; double taps guarded; pedals let go when the thumb slides off.
- **Tested in Play:** new player on a test save (`CityLegends_ClaudeTest1`): pick, start, rejoin, the free car stays gone. Test drive there and back. GET OUT while rolling. Sea reset. A car onto 5 lots (biggest step 0.11). Down a High garage ramp. A lamp knocked down. Touch mode forced on at PC size: no errors, layout right. 0 script errors.

## Traffic split and optimise (2026-11-04)
- **Owner:** "reduce a little bit of traffic on the road near the harbour and add some traffic on the highway", "add a little more cars on the highway (not alot) and make them go 120 kmh", "split some cars from the casnio road onto the harbour road, so its not too busy", "optimize the game. The game is a little glitchy".
- **Highway:** it was empty round you: the cap of 10 counted cars far away, and those couldn't be moved to you because each counted against itself. Fixed; cap 12 (`Config.Traffic.RivieraHighwayMax`). Checked: 12 cars within 700 studs on the Corniche, about 95 studs a second = 120 km/h (`RivieraHighwayMph`, the proper highways; Summit Pass and Serpent Road keep their own pace).
- **Casino and harbour:** the casino road is the Town Road, the harbour road the Coast Road on the front plus Harbour Street. `Config.Traffic.BusyZones`: harbour at most 13 cars, casino 8. A stretch over its number sends a car out of your sight somewhere else round you (at the casino: mostly down to the harbour). Harbour Street and Casino Climb take a share of cars (`StreetShareBy`, `SplitTurnK`). Measured over 14 s: parked at the casino 15.4 -> 12.4 cars on its stretch, at the harbour 10.6 on the front and 7.4 at the casino. The first cap alone (12 at the harbour) only pushed cars onto the casino road.
- **Optimise:** measured with `ScriptProfilerService` (`ClientStart(player, 1000)`, `ClientStop`, `ClientRequestData`, `OnNewData`, `DeserializeJSON`). Same test drive on the Coast Road, 14 s in Studio: 48-50 fps with 8-10 frames over 50 ms (worst 113 ms) -> 113 fps, none over 50 ms (worst 45 ms). Causes: every road piece a car reached for the first time scanned all the Riviera's pieces (`conflictsOf`; now a grid); LOD groups: at most 3 in or out per frame, sweep 1/16 per frame, no lookup of a far stand-in's building when it is past range. 32,161 thin road parts stopped casting shadows (the newer roads never had that off; tall pillars keep theirs). Parked at the harbour the frame rate was fine already: 146 fps, shadows off 165, post effects no change.
- **Not checked:** a real phone. Client held 150-220k parts while driving; the map is 361k parts (Roads 183k).
- New imports (2026-11-05, owner): Murcielago LP650-4 Roadster (replaces the LP640's model; the Fast & Furious file is dropped) + LP670-4 SV, Urus Performante, Ferrari California, 458 Spider, 458 Speciale; same routine as the Lamborghinis, every bad cluster reported to the owner and rebuilt 1:1 from their photo, NC licences flagged. Prompt: `Prompts/New imports - Murcielago, Urus Performante, Ferraris.md`.

    - **Traps found:** a 512 x 1024 picture as a SurfaceAppearance ColorMap comes out white (the Spider's whole cabin): use a square picture (768 x 768 works). A pack with several cars gives `Wheels.001`, `FL.002` and so on: strip every `.00n` or the game finds no wheels. Check a palette swap by part name: each part has its own uploaded copy of the picture. Studio's `execute_luau` HTTP refuses raw.githubusercontent.com now too ("not in the Assistant HTTP allowlist"): copy scripts with `multi_edit` or a long string in `execute_luau`.
